### [1] [Effects of Quantum Spin-Connection Foam in the Solar System, Galaxies, and the Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12404)
*Igor V. Kanatchikov,Valery A. Kholodnyi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文提出量子自旋连接泡沫（量子引力在广义相对论预正则量子化框架下的表现）可能已在宇宙学常数和小加速度下的牛顿动力学修正（星系平坦旋转曲线）中被观察到。作者推导出修正后的牛顿势，引入基本小加速度尺度 a_* = 8πGħϰ，并将 ϰ 与标准模型纯杨-米尔斯扇区的质量间隙的强子尺度联系起来，得到与MOND的Milgrom加速度尺度相容的估计值；同时通过 a_*² 与宇宙学常数的联系获得合理的宇宙学常数值。论文在非相对论近似下从预正则量子引力的最简解推导出Milgrom型MOND及一个理论上不同的插值函数，并指出这些效应可能在太阳系乃至实验室实验中可观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在论证广义相对论预正则量子化所描述的量子自旋连接泡沫效应可能已在现有观测现象（平坦星系旋转曲线、宇宙学常数）中显现，从而为量子引力理论提供观测检验途径，并统一解释MOND修正动力学与暗能量问题。

Method: 论文基于广义相对论的预正则量子化（precanonical quantization）理论框架，通过引入维度为逆空间体积的参数 ϰ，构建基本小加速度尺度 a_* = 8πGħϰ。在非相对论近似下，从预正则量子引力的最简解计算平均场加速度，进而修正牛顿势和牛顿动力学。通过将 ϰ 与标准模型纯杨-米尔斯扇区质量间隙的强子尺度以及 a_*² 与宇宙学常数的关系进行建联来确定参数数值。

Result: 研究得到：(1) 修正的牛顿势包含了基本小加速度尺度 a_*，在加速度远小于 a_* 时修正动力学趋向Milgromian MOND行为；(2) 通过 ϰ 与强子质量间隙尺度的联系，估计的 a_* 与MOND中的Milgrom加速度尺度相容；(3) a_*² 与宇宙学常数之间的关联给出了现实可行的宇宙学常数值；(4) 推导出Milgromian MOND及其一个理论上独立的插值函数，该插值函数源于预正则量子引力的非相对论平均场近似；(5) 预测修正的牛顿动力学效应可能在太阳系乃至实验室尺度上可观测。

Conclusion: 论文结论认为量子自旋连接泡沫的效应可能已在宇宙学常数和星系旋转曲线中体现，预正则量子引力为MOND现象和暗能量提供统一的量子引力理论解释。该框架产生的加速度修正不仅在星系尺度有效，还可能在太阳系和实验室实验中检验，为量子引力理论的观测验证开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We argue that effects of the quantum spin-connection foam, which describes quantum gravity according to the precanonical quantization of General Relativity, may already be observed in the form of the small cosmological constant and a modification of Newtonian dynamics at small accelerations, manifested in the flat rotation curves of galaxies. We obtain a modification of the Newtonian potential that takes into account the existence of a fundamental small acceleration scale, $a_* = 8πG\hbar\varkappa$, where $\varkappa$ is a parameter with the dimensions of inverse spatial volume that appears on dimensional grounds. The connection between $\varkappa$ and the hadronic scale of the mass gap in the pure Yang-Mills sector of the Standard Model leads to an estimated value of $a_*$ compatible with the Milgromian acceleration scale in MOND. The connection between $a_*^2$ and the cosmological constant leads to a realistic value of the latter. Milgromian MOND, together with a theoretically distinct interpolating function, is derived under the assumption that classical dynamics is modified by the mean-field acceleration calculated from the simplest solution of precanonical quantum gravity in the nonrelativistic approximation. We also indicate that the effects of Newtonian dynamics modified by the spin-connection foam may be observable in the Solar System and even in laboratory experiments.

</details>


### [2] [On Super-Hyperbolic Wormhole Geometries and their Deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12412)
*Subrat Parida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个统一的几何与相对论框架，基于超双曲形变的经典悬链面（catenoid）构造新型可穿越虫洞，引入局部曲率参数κ和基于Mittag-Leffler函数的超双曲变形函数，在α→1⁺极限下恢复经典悬链面几何，并推导了形状函数、曲率张量、物质源、薄壳运动方程及稳定性判据的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿越虫洞模型多依赖特定的几何构造或人工物质场，缺乏统一的几何框架来系统地生成并检验具有物理可行性的虫洞解。本文旨在通过一种新的超双曲变形家族，扩展虫洞理论，并为可穿越且局部稳定的虫洞配置提供数学自洽的描述。

Method: 采用超双曲变形对经典悬链面进行推广，引入局部曲率参数κ以保障喉部正则性，并利用基于Mittag-Leffler函数的超双曲形变函数𝔖C_α(v)（变形参数α>1控制径向起始与远场增长）。在非恒定红移函数Φ(r)条件下，系统推导封闭解析表达式，包括形状函数、曲率张量、物质源和衔接条件，最终得到精确的薄壳运动方程与稳定性判据。

Result: 得到了完整且封闭的解析框架：包括形状函数、曲率张量、物质源及薄壳动力学方程和稳定性准则。该框架在α→1⁺边界极限下连续逼近经典悬链面几何，并识别出可穿越虫洞保持局部稳定的条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为虫洞理论提供了数学一致且物理可行的推广框架，开辟了在广义相对论中探索奇异几何的新途径，并明确了可穿越虫洞在何种条件下能保持局部稳定性。

Abstract: In this study, we developed a unified geometric and relativistic framework for a new family of traversable wormholes constructed from a super-hyperbolic deformation of the classical catenoid, whose geometric embedding combines an explicit local curvature parameter $κ$, which secures a regular throat, with the Mittag-Leffler-based super-hyperboloid deformation function ${\mathfrak{S}C}_α (v)$, where the deformation parameter $α>1$ governs the radial onset and far-field growth of the embedding beyond the throat, continuously approaching the classical catenoidal geometry in the boundary limit $α\rightarrow1^{+}$. Allowing a non-constant redshift $Φ(r)$, we systematically evaluate closed analytical expressions for the shape function, curvature tensors, matter sources, and junction conditions, culminating in an exact thin-shell equation of motion and stability criterion. This work establishes a mathematically consistent and physically viable framework for the extension of wormhole theory, providing a new avenue for exploring exotic geometries within general relativity and identifying the conditions under which traversable configurations remain locally stable.

</details>


### [3] [Rainbow McVittie Horizons in an Expanding Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12417)
*Amani Ashour,Ahmed Farag Ali*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在Gravity's Rainbow框架下推导了空间平坦McVittie时空的Friedmann动力学与视界热力学，构造了有效彩虹哈勃率的非吸积理想流体扇区，并通过32个宇宙学计时器数据与1580条Pantheon+光曲线（含完整协方差矩阵）结合Planck物质密度先验进行拟合，得到H0=68.75±2.63 km/s/Mpc、Ωm=0.3157±0.0070、ε=0.0106，验证了小晚期彩虹修正的观测可行性与广义相对论极限的偏好性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在引力彩虹（Gravity's Rainbow）框架下，McVittie时空的Friedmann动力学和视界热力学关系尚未被系统推导。该工作旨在建立这一时空下自洽的热力学系统，并通过宇宙学观测数据约束低能彩虹形变，检验彩虹修正是否与现有观测相容。

Method: 推导空间平坦McVittie时空在Gravity's Rainbow中的Einstein方程、俘获视界、表面引力、Misner-Sharp能量、统一第一定律及Clausius关系，构成闭合热力学/动力学系统。引入低能彩虹形变参数ε，利用32个宇宙学计时器测量和1580条Pantheon+光曲线（含完整协方差矩阵）并结合Planck物质密度先验进行最大似然拟合。

Result: 拟合给出H0=68.75±2.63 km/s/Mpc，Ωm=0.3157±0.0070，ε=0.0106(+0.0234/-0.0231)。结果表明小的晚期彩虹修正在观测上是可行的，且广义相对论极限落在优选参数区域内。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Gravity's Rainbow中McVittie时空自洽的热力学-动力学闭环框架，并通过多数据集联合约束证明了小幅度晚期彩虹修正与当前观测数据兼容，为超出广义相对论的修正引力模型提供了一个观测上受支持的实现方案。

Abstract: We derive the Friedmann dynamics and apparent-horizon thermodynamics of a spatially flat McVittie spacetime in Gravity's Rainbow and construct a consistent non-accreting perfect-fluid sector with an effective rainbow Hubble rate. The Einstein equations, trapping horizon, surface gravity, Misner--Sharp energy, unified first law, and Clausius relation form a closed thermodynamic system reproducing the Friedmann dynamics. We then introduce a low-energy rainbow deformation and constrain it using 32 cosmic-chronometer measurements and 1580 Pantheon+ light curves with the full covariance matrix. Combined with the Planck matter-density prior, the fit gives $H_0=68.75\pm2.63~\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, $Ω_m=0.3157\pm0.0070$, and $ε=0.0106^{+0.0234}_{-0.0231}$. The resulting constraints establish the observational viability of small late-time rainbow corrections and place the general-relativistic limit within the preferred parameter region.

</details>


### [4] [Phase-plane formulation of weak gravitational deflection in static spherical spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12423)
*Reggie C. Pantig,Ali Övgün*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种静态球对称黑洞（以Schwarzschild时空为主）引力光偏折的相平面表述方法。通过振幅与内禀相位表示光子轨道，利用拉格朗日反演推导出弱偏折系数的全阶显式公式，解释了Schwarzschild级数中交替理性项与π依赖项的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弱偏折计算依赖对零测地线轨迹的微扰并定位出射渐近线，结构复杂且难以直观理解偏折级数系数的代数/超越结构。本文旨在建立一种更自然的相平面框架，使偏折角的系数结构（有理数与π项交替出现）能获得清晰的物理解释。

Method: 将光子轨道表示为振幅与内禀相位两个变量；对于Schwarzschild时空，精确径向首积分将振幅演化约化为三次代数关系。物理分支通过单次逆代数映射生成局域相位因子，进而利用拉格朗日反演获得弱偏折系数在M/b幂次下的全阶显式公式。每个系数分解为一个代数相位贡献和一个普适三角矩。

Result: 推导出Schwarzschild弱偏折展开的全阶系数公式，每个系数由代数相位贡献与普适三角矩构成，系统解释了级数中有理数与π依赖项交替出现的结构。与精确径向散射积分及传统轨道微扰方法相比，得到的标准弱偏折系数完全一致。该框架可推广至一般静态球对称几何，且相位映射的支点奇异性恰好对应临界光子轨道，控制弱偏折展开的收敛半径。

Conclusion: 相平面表述为引力光偏折提供了新的理论视角，不仅统一了Schwarzschild情形下偏折级数的代数与超越结构来源，还通过支点奇异性将弱偏折展开的收敛性与临界光子轨道建立内在联系，并具备推广到更一般静态球对称时空的普适性。

Abstract: This paper develops a phase-plane formulation of gravitational light deflection by static and spherically symmetric black holes, with Schwarzschild spacetime as the principal case. Instead of perturbing the null trajectory and locating the displaced outgoing asymptote, we represent the orbit through an amplitude and an intrinsic phase. The bending angle then follows from the excess physical azimuth accumulated while the intrinsic phase advances between two fixed asymptotic endpoints. For Schwarzschild spacetime, the exact radial first integral reduces the amplitude evolution to a cubic algebraic relation. Its physical branch generates the local phase factor through a single inverse algebraic map. Lagrange inversion then yields an explicit all-order weak-deflection coefficient formula in powers of the invariant ratio \(M/b\). Each coefficient separates into an algebraic phase contribution and a universal trigonometric moment, which explains the alternating rational and \(π\)-dependent structure of the Schwarzschild series. Independent comparison with the exact radial scattering integral and conventional orbit perturbation reproduces the standard weak-bending coefficients. The same phase framework extends to general static spherical geometries, while the Schwarzschild cubic represents an especially simple member of a broader algebraic class. The branch singularity of the phase map also coincides with the critical photon orbit and governs the convergence of the weak-deflection expansion.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖锐类时Israel匹配在维里边界处产生负表面层，这是尖锐构造中遗漏的环境补偿的分布表示。作者希望用有限宽度、背景相对欠密而局部静止系能量密度保持为正的源来代替这种零宽度负源，从而消除分布层并对环境补偿进行更真实的建模。

Method: 采用普通分支上的类时Israel匹配以识别出负表面层问题；将其替换为有限宽度、背景相对欠密（但局部静止系能量密度为正）的源；构造满足Hamiltonian与动量约束的共形平坦、常平均曲率（CMC）ADM初始切片；通过分辨率细化验证残差收敛；在有限半径处确保几何与物质变量回到局部有效FLRW数据的容差范围内；外部区域由精确解析的局部有效FLRW解定义；保持GCT框架作为全局动机，但本计算严格限定为经典GR计算，c0和G0固定不变。

Result: 所构造的切片满足ADM的Hamiltonian与动量约束，残差随分辨率细化收敛；在有限半径处几何与物质变量在声明容差内回到局部有效FLRW数据；切片不含残余分布层，并满足重建后的弱能量条件、零能量条件和主能量条件。

Conclusion: 通过以物理正的有限宽度源替代尖锐构造中的分布负表面层，可以在经典广义相对论框架内构造出光滑、物理自洽的维里化晕初始数据切片，为后续演化提供更可靠的起点。

Abstract: Within classical general relativity, we compare a sharp timelike junction and a smooth finite-width spacelike initial-data embedding of a positive-excess virialized halo in a homogeneous Lambda-FLRW environment. On the ordinary branch, timelike Israel matching at the virial boundary produces a negative surface layer: a distributional representation of the environmental compensation omitted by the sharp construction. We replace this zero-width source by a finite-width, background-relative underdensity whose local rest-frame energy density remains positive. The resulting conformally flat, constant-mean-curvature ADM initial slice satisfies the Hamiltonian and momentum constraints, with the residuals converging under resolution refinement. At finite radius, its geometric and matter variables return to the local-effective FLRW data within the declared tolerances: the constructed slice contains no residual distributional layer and satisfies the reconstructed weak, null, and dominant energy conditions. For radii beyond the numerical endpoint, the exterior is defined by the exact analytic local-effective FLRW solution. The GCT framework supplies the global motivation clock interpretation, while the present calculations are local-effective classical-GR calculations with the bound-sector constants c0 and G0 held fixed: no radial GCT lapse or constant interpolation is constructed.

</details>

*Balázs Endre Szigeti,Imre Ferenc Barna,Gergely Gábor Barnaföldi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对暗流体模型进行了稳定性分析，该模型被描述为非相对论性、旋转、无粘性、自引力流体，采用多方状态方程和球对称假设。通过Guderley-Landau-Stanyukovich问题的自相似ansatz求解耦合非线性偏微分方程组，发现四个Lyapunov指数中三个为负、一个为正，且该正指数较小，表明自相似解仅具有弱不稳定性，在考察区间内保持动力学稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在对暗流体模型进行严格的稳定性分析，以评估自相似解在相空间中的动力学行为，判断该解是否在扰动下保持稳健，从而验证模型作为暗物质/暗能量候选者的合理性。

Method: 将暗流体建模为非相对论、旋转、无粘性、自引力流体，采用球对称假设和多方状态方程。通过Guderley-Landau-Stanyukovich自相似ansatz将耦合非线性偏微分方程组进行求解，并通过计算Lyapunov指数来量化相空间中解的稳定性。

Result: 四个Lyapunov指数中三个为负、一个为正，表明相空间中存在一个不稳定方向和三个收缩方向。正Lyapunov指数数值相对较小，说明自相似解仅弱不稳定，在考察的时间/参数区间内能够保持动力学稳健性。

Conclusion: 该暗流体自相似解整体上是动态稳健的，只有一个弱不稳定方向，这意味着该模型在相当大的参数/时间范围内仍可作为有效的暗流体描述，但其长期稳定性仍需关注唯一的不稳定模式。

Abstract: We present a stability analysis of a dark-fluid model described as a non-relativistic, rotating, non-viscous, self-gravitating fluid. We assume spherical symmetry and model the matter by a polytropic equation of state. The resulting coupled nonlinear partial differential equation system is solved using a self-similar ansatz known from the Guderley-Landau-Stanyukovich problem. We find that three of the four Lyapunov exponents are negative, while only one is positive. This indicates one unstable direction and three contracting directions in phase space. The single positive exponent is relatively small, suggesting that the self-similar solution is only weakly unstable and remains dynamically robust over the investigated interval.

</details>


### [7] [Thermal false vacuum decay near black holes is aspherical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12469)
*D. S. Gorbunov,D. G. Levkov,V. E. Maslov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究标量场假真空在(3+1)维Schwarzschild黑洞（与Hawking温度的周围环境达到热平衡）附近的衰变问题。标量场模型具有负四次自耦合，类似于标准模型Higgs sector在大场极限下的行为。研究表明，若黑洞不是太小，假真空会以非球对称方式衰变：通过事件视界外侧形成的真真空泡扩张来实现。具体识别出三种衰变机制：最大最冷的黑洞以量子隧穿为主（无限薄bounce位于视界某点）；中等质量黑洞由热涨落主导产生非球对称临界泡；接近最小黑洞时，热涨落仍主导衰变但临界泡呈球对称并覆盖整个视界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在黑洞存在的情况下假真空衰变的物理过程，特别是黑洞Hawking辐射和引力场如何影响真空衰变机制和临界泡的对称性，这对于理解Higgs势不稳定性和早期宇宙物理具有重要意义。

Abstract: In this work, we establish a systematic framework to describe the vacuum decays in a radiation-dominated FLRW universe from the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism. As a phenomenological example, we consider a polynomial potential and study the structure of the memory kernel as well as the correlation functions of the noise term. With a less restrictive scale split by allowing $φ$ to carry spatial dependence, further extensions remain possible to describe the whole dynamics of cosmological first-order phase transitions via numerical simulations.

Furthermore, the Landauer spectrum obtained by quantizing the area decreases as the quantum number associated with the horizon increases, although this variation does not occur in the same way in the SEC and DEC branches. By modeling the constellation as a Cellular Sheaf and applying Topological Data Analysis (TDA), our protocol derives a public acceptance event ($Ω$) exclusively from classical beacon telemetry. To rigorously validate this mechanism, we develop a modular simulation framework equipped with stochastic noise injection and an explicit GEAT security ledger. At 2,000 km, the median conditional candidate rates are $2.14 \times 10^{-6}$ and $5.87 \times 10^{-7}$ bit per emitted pulse, respectively; both configurations return zero at 3,000-5,000 km. TDA is active in all 4,788 evaluated windows, but does not extend the positive-candidate range in this scenario. These exported rates are conditional numerical candidates: the full protocol-level composable-security proof remains incomplete and the certified composable rate is therefore zero throughout.

Quantum optimization offers an alternative for exploring this combinatorial partition space. The proposed framework provides a feasible and scalable pathway for quantum optimization in large-scale power systems.

Abstract: Quantum subspace diagonalization methods are promising algorithms for quantum chemistry on near-term quantum computers. Abstract: Ensuring ancilla safety is a critical correctness requirement for quantum compilation, since ancilla qubits are routinely introduced to implement complex operations with fewer gates and reduced depth. Abstract: Determining whether a target unitary can be implemented within a prescribed quantum circuit architecture is a fundamental problem in quantum information, with direct implications for optimisation and compilation of quantum circuits, and hardware-efficient quantum computation. While existing synthesis and compilation methods are primarily constructive, they generally do not provide rigorous certificates that a unitary cannot be realised using given implementation resources. Here we introduce a general framework to define quantum circuit architecture witnesses, which certify the incompatibility of a unitary transformation with a specified quantum circuit architecture. Abstract: Magic plays a dual role in quantum computation: it promotes stabilizer dynamics from efficient classical simulability to universality, but it presents a central challenge for fault tolerance, since non-stabilizer operations are harder to protect against noise. Abstract: Unitary designs provide finite-moment approximations to Haar-random unitaries, with wide-ranging applications across physics and quantum information, from scrambling and black-hole dynamics to foundational primitives in quantum algorithms. Motivated by the physical requirement that scrambling arise within the system itself, Schuster, Ma, Lombardi, Brandão, and Huang (arXiv:2509.26310) left open whether strong unitary designs can be generated in logarithmic depth using only the system qubits. Abstract: Quantum technology has the potential to transform scientific discovery, but quantum advantages often require processing capabilities well beyond the reach of experimental platforms. Q$Ψ$ extends beyond the regimes captured by quantum Fisher information and provides a framework for systematically identifying rigorous quantum advantages in practical experimental tasks. Abstract: Characterising the efficiency with which designs over various subsets of the unitary group may be constructed is an important goal of quantum information theory. While it is now known that approximate unitary designs can be realised in depth logarithmic in the system size, it has recently been shown that ensembles of local nearest-neighbour sublinear-depth one-dimensional circuits over the matchgate, orthogonal, and symplectic groups cannot form approximate 2-designs over their parent groups; similarly, sublinear-depth ensembles of Cliffords cannot form a Clifford 4-design. In this note we show that this remarkable exponential separation is not merely an artefact of restricting to ensembles consisting of unitaries from the subgroups themselves, but rather that no ensemble of local nearest-neighbour sublinear-depth unitaries can realise approximate designs in the aforementioned cases, even when employing "ambient" unitaries from beyond the subgroup itself (possibly acting on ancilla qubits). This implies that various natural tomography and benchmarking schemes which involves sampling from these groups suffer from a dramatic circuit depth overhead compared to similar protocols which involve sampling from the full unitary group. We additionally conclude that, in all of the above cases, the known linear-depth design constructions are up to constant factors optimal.

</details>

Method: 1. 针对一般核的VIDEs，在记忆项强度参数M<1条件下开发高效量子算法；2. 针对M≥1的一般核情形给出下界，证明该问题对一类系统不可解；3. 对于可简洁分解为指数形式的结构核函数，即使在M≥1时也能通过将VIDE转化为更大规模的ODE集合（称为"马尔可夫化"过程）实现高效量子算法。

Result: 算法输出编码时间区间或特定时刻状态描述的量子态，在给定问题输入的高效电路后，相比经典算法在系统规模上实现指数级加速。同时提供了M≥1时一般核VIDEs的难解性下界，并通过马尔可夫化在结构化核上绕过了这一障碍。

Abstract: Existing quantum algorithms for simulating dynamical systems -- from Hamiltonian simulation to linear and nonlinear differential equations solvers -- simulate Markovian dynamics, in which the system's future evolution depends solely on its current state. We turn our attention to developing quantum algorithms for non-Markovian dynamical systems where the system's future evolution depends on its past history and thus has memory. Specifically, we develop efficient algorithms for linear Volterra integro-differential equations (VIDEs) with a convolution memory kernel that output a quantum state encoding the state description over a time interval or at a particular time. Given efficient circuits for the problem inputs, our algorithms achieve an exponential speedup in system size over existing classical algorithms.
  We develop an algorithm for general kernels assuming that $\textsf{M} < 1$, where $\textsf{M}$ characterizes the strength of the memory term relative to the dissipation of the Markovian part of the dynamics. We complement this with lower bounds for general-kernel VIDEs when $\textsf{M} \geq 1$, showing that the problem becomes intractable for a family of systems. However, by specializing to structured kernels which admit concise decompositions over exponentials, we develop efficient quantum algorithms even when $\textsf M \geq 1$ by converting the VIDE into a larger set of ODEs, a procedure which we call Markovianization. As an application of the overall framework, we discuss the Mori-Zwanzig formalism used in open quantum systems and fluid dynamics. Overall, our results expand the range of dynamical systems that quantum computers can simulate efficiently.

</details>

Abstract: To achieve large-scale fault-tolerant quantum computation, it may be easier to combine many small sets of qubits than to construct a single large set. For example via quantum error correction with block codes, or distributed quantum processors utilizing shared entanglement. In these regimes, the time or error budget of the overall quantum computation may be dominated by non-local operations. Hence, it is worthwhile to minimize the number of these operations. We consider the case where both non-local and local connectivity may be arbitrarily restricted, and give an asymptotically optimal synthesis method for distributed CNOT and Clifford circuits, based on block-matrix Gaussian elimination. We extend this to all Clifford+RZ circuits by generalizing the Pauli exponential circuit representation; this naturally integrates with existing methods for optimizing T-count. As an application, we show how to implement CNOT circuits in a CSS code encoding n logical qubits in k blocks using O(nk) inter-block transversal CNOTs and intra-block Pauli measurements.

Result: 实验实现了平均窃听者检测概率为0.15±0.02（每个共享量子比特），窃听检测通过控制量子比特的测量完成，无需公开或丢弃密钥材料。虽然当前实现因量子SWITCH内部测量所需的后选择而不构成安全的QKD协议，但提供了利用无限因果序在不牺牲密钥位情况下检测窃听的原理验证。

Conclusion: 该工作是无限因果序作为量子资源在密码学中应用的首次实验演示，证明了将Alice和Bob嵌入量子SWITCH可以检测窃听而不泄露密钥比特。尽管仍存在后选择等限制，它为未来在更完善架构中实现安全QKD奠定了基础，展示了一个与标准BB84范式截然不同的窃听检测途径。

Abstract: In quantum physics the order in which different operations occur can be placed in superposition. The resulting processes have an indefinite causal order and are both of fundamental interest and can be viewed as a novel quantum resource that enables a variety of new protocols. Here we report an experimental implementation of one such protocol, where we perform BB84-like quantum cryptography by placing Alice and Bob's measurement-and-preparation operations in a photonic quantum SWITCH. By embedding Alice and Bob within the quantum SWITCH, the protocol achieves an average eavesdropper detection probability of $0.15 \pm 0.02$ per shared qubit, with eavesdropper detection performed through measurements of the control qubit rather than by comparing the key. Unlike the standard BB84 and related schemes, which detect eavesdropping by publicly revealing and discarding a fraction of the raw key, our approach requires no disclosure of key material: every retained qubit can, in principle, be tested for eavesdropping while remaining available for key generation. The experiment relies on a new measurement technique that allows the polarization of a photon to be measured inside the quantum SWITCH without destroying path coherence. Although the present implementation does not yet constitute a secure quantum key distribution protocol, owing to the post-selection required for measurements within the quantum SWITCH, it provides a proof of principle that indefinite causal order can be exploited to detect eavesdropping without sacrificing key bits.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过加强Oguiso-Peternell定理，利用拓扑不动点理论、变换群方法以及基本群的不变量分析，分别从正性和负性两个方向建立Calabi-Yau流形的拓扑刚性与排除性障碍。

Abstract: We study whether the topology underlying a Calabi-Yau manifold can support natural geometric positivity or negativity structures. In even complex dimension $n \geq 4$ (assuming $b_2=1$ when $n \geq 6$), we strengthen a theorem of Oguiso-Peternell by proving that a Calabi-Yau manifold is not homeomorphic to a weak Fano $n$-fold. The same obstruction applies to Kähler manifolds with quasi-positive holomorphic sectional curvature. A transformation-group analogue excludes, in particular, symplectic manifolds admitting Hamiltonian circle actions with isolated fixed points. On the negative side, we show that the fundamental group of a Calabi-Yau manifold is not isomorphic to that of a Kähler hyperbolic manifold. Taken together, these results exhibit a common topological rigidity separating Calabi-Yau manifolds from several fundamental classes governed by geometric positivity or negativity.

</details>

Abstract: The main result of this article shows that the quiver mutation class of a malleable real Morsification uniquely determines the integral monodromy module of a plane curve singularity. In particular, we show that the quiver mutation class of a malleable divide determines the complex topological type of an irreducible plane curve singularity. This establishes the algebraic-to-topological implication of a conjecture of S.~Fomin, P.~Pylyavskyy, E.~Shustin and D.~Thurston in the malleable irreducible case. The result is proven by developing representation-theoretic techniques based on an equivariant Euler pairing in the derived category of continuous finite-dimensional dg modules over a differential bigraded Ginzburg algebra. A key step uses these techniques to show that the quiver mutation class of a plabic fence uniquely recovers the torsion part of the Alexander module of the associated smooth link.

</details>

Abstract: We establish lower bounds for the Smith--Thom deficiency of the Hilbert square of a maximal nonsingular real projective variety. Abstract: We study descendant integrals on moduli stacks of simplicially stable curves. Motivation: 基于Jacobian猜想（Keller映射）的研究背景，作者希望从碰撞（collision）这一初等对象出发，通过极化代数工具给出压缩原理的规范构造，既解释已知的24到20维压缩，又测试Zhao消失猜想的有效性。

Result: Thompson映射的增长序列为2,4,11,20,20，碰撞生成的子代数恰为MacFarlane 20维不变子空间；给出了350单项式齐次四次式使Zhao消失猜想失败，并在Q(i)上给出精确梯度碰撞；在Q、Q(i)、C上，包含该碰撞的恰当不变线性子空间在对称提升不变之前不存在。

Conclusion: 碰撞生成的压缩原理给出了已有24到20压缩的规范解释，同时构造的反例表明Zhao消失猜想不成立，但作者不声称全局极小性，只强调精确计算的可验证性。

Abstract: We formulate an elementary collision-generated compression principle for homogeneous Keller maps. In the language of polarization algebras, the construction takes the subalgebra generated by a collision; the additional point is that this subalgebra carries a noninjective Keller restriction and controls the dimension of the standard symmetric lift. For Thompson's 24-variable cubic-homogeneous map, exact polarization gives growth \(2,4,11,20,20\), and the resulting subalgebra is exactly MacFarlane's 20-dimensional invariant subspace. Thus the known \(24\)-to-\(20\) compression is recovered canonically from the collision itself. Although the resulting 40-variable lift had already been noted, we give an explicit 350-monomial homogeneous quartic for which Zhao's Vanishing Conjecture fails, together with an exact gradient collision over \(\Q(i)\). Over \(\Q\), \(\Q(i)\), and \(\C\), no proper invariant linear subspace containing this collision is available before the unchanged symmetric lift. No global minimality is claimed. The principal explicit calculations are checked by an accompanying exact calculation.

</details>


### [85] [Rigidity for Lie algebras of locally finite derivations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12567)
*Mohamed Ali Belabbas*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在特征零域上有限生成的交换代数A上，所有元素在A上局部有限的有限生成李子代数（属于导子代数Der_K(A)）必为有限维。进而，由有限个局部有限导子生成的李子代数中，"有限维"、"在A上局部有限作用"和"所有元素局部有限"三个性质等价。关键的第二定理是：所有元素局部幂零的李子代数必可解，当A约化时其导长度至多为dim A且该界是紧的/最优的。对仿射簇X的代数闭域情形，推导出由有限个连通代数子群生成的Aut(X)的子群是代数的当且仅当其切代数生成的李代数的每个元素均局部有限。这回答了Popov在2005年提出的关于两个幺幂单参数子群的问题，并刻画了允许通过包含状态坐标的多项式可观测量实现有限维双线性精确实现的二元多项式控制系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于几个相互关联的经典问题：Popov于2005年提出的关于由幺幂单参数子群生成的代数群子群是否为代数的问题；局部有限导子与局部幂零导子之间的结构关系；以及多项式控制系统的有限维双线性实现问题。核心在于理解Der_K(A)中由局部有限元素生成的李子代数的维数结构——此前人们知道局部幂零导子生成可解李代数，但局部有限导子的情形尚不清楚，本文旨在弥合这一理论空白。

Method: 证明的核心技术是引入一个"共有限理想"（cofinite ideal），该理想与Spec A的每个结合点的闭包相交。在沿对应有限子概形二阶消失的导子构成的子代数上，局部有限性被强制转化为局部幂零性。因此该子代数与L的交集由第二定理得到是可解的，且在L中具有有限余维数；结合伴随作用的局部有限性，最终推导出L的有限维性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 特征零域上有限生成交换代数A的Der_K(A)中，所有元素局部有限的有限生成李子代数必为有限维；(2) 所有元素局部幂零的李子代数可解，当A约化时导长度上界为dim A且该界是紧的/最优的；(3) 对仿射簇X，由有限个连通代数子群生成的Aut(X)子群为代数的充要条件是相应李代数每个元素局部有限，这回答了Popov 2005年的问题；(4) 给出了允许精确有限维双线性实现的二元多项式控制系统的刻画。

Conclusion: 本文通过精妙的代数几何与李代数技术（共有限理想与局部有限/幂零导子之间的对应关系），建立了一组深刻的定理，将局部有限导子与局部幂零导子的结构理论统一起来，并以此回答了代数群论中十余年悬而未决的Popov问题，同时为控制论中的实现理论提供了代数刻画。这些结果展示了导子代数结构、群作用代数性与系统实现理论之间深刻的关联。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a finitely generated commutative algebra over a field $\mathbb K$ of characteristic zero. We prove that every finitely generated Lie subalgebra $L\subseteq\operatorname{Der}_{\mathbb K}(A)$ whose elements are locally finite on $A$ is finite-dimensional. Consequently, for a Lie subalgebra generated by finitely many locally finite derivations, the following are equivalent: it is finite-dimensional, it acts locally finitely on $A$, and all its elements are locally finite. A key ingredient is a second theorem of independent interest: every Lie subalgebra of $\operatorname{Der}_{\mathbb K}(A)$ whose elements are locally nilpotent is solvable; when $A$ is reduced, its derived length is at most $\dim A$, and this bound is sharp. For an affine variety $X$ over an algebraically closed field, we deduce that a subgroup of $\operatorname{Aut}(X)$ generated by finitely many connected algebraic subgroups is algebraic if and only if every element of the Lie algebra generated by their tangent algebras is locally finite. For two unipotent one-parameter subgroups, this provides an answer to a problem posed by Popov in 2005. Our results also characterize polynomial control systems admitting an exact finite-dimensional bilinear realization by polynomial observables containing the state coordinates. The proofs rest on the introduction of a cofinite ideal meeting the closure of every associated point of $\operatorname{Spec} A$: on the subalgebra of derivations vanishing to second order along the corresponding finite subscheme, local finiteness forces local nilpotence. The intersection of that subalgebra with $L$ is therefore solvable by the second theorem, and has finite codimension in $L$; together with local finiteness of the adjoint action, this yields finite-dimensionality.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性空间上的驯化平展基本群此前缺乏系统研究，本文旨在引入并证明其在拓扑有限生成及有限呈现方面的性质，为刚性解析几何中的基本群理论提供基础工具。

Method: 采用对数几何技术（将对数结构推广到有限生成幺半群之外），建立驯化log平展基本群的类似有限性命题；同时利用adic空间映射的"垂直紧化"技巧来处理几何结构的紧化问题，从而推导出pi_1^t(X/K)的拓扑有限性质。

Result: 证明了在qcqs且K的驯化Galois群拓扑有限生成的条件下，π₁ᵗ(X/K)拓扑有限生成；在严格半稳定形式概形的刚性一般纤维且特殊纤维光滑闭包有射影snc紧化的条件下，π₁ᵗ(X/K)拓扑有限呈现。

Conclusion: 驯化平展基本群在适度的有限性假设下具有良好的拓扑性质，对数几何与adic空间紧化方法为这类解析基本群的有限性研究提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We introduce the tame étale fundamental group $π_1^t(X/K)$ of a rigid space X over a non-archimedean field K. We show that if X is qcqs and K has topologically finitely generated tame Galois group (e.g. algebraically closed or a local field), then $π_1^t(X/K)$ is topologically finitely generated. If X is moreover the rigid generic fibre of a strictly semistable formal scheme such that the smooth locus of its special fibre admits a projective snc compactification, then $π_1^t(X/K)$ is topologically finitely presented. The proofs rely on techniques of logarithmic geometry (extended beyond its usual scope of finitely generated monoids), in particular on an analogous finiteness statement for the tame log étale fundamental group, and on the 'vertical compactification' of a map of adic spaces.

</details>


### [87] [Supersymmetry, differential operators of infinite order and theta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12846)
*Mikhail Kapranov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文通过超对称性重新诠释了Sato等人提出的关于Theta零值（Thetanullwerte）模形式性的无穷阶微分算子（DOI）方法。作者发现，任何奇超对称生成元的朴素指数即为DOI，而亏格n的Riemann theta函数由超群OSp(1|2n)（及元形变覆盖）作用于Siegel平面的自然超加厚来支配。n=2时对应三维N=1超共形群，结构恰为自由无质量标量超多重态；n>2时得到Lagrangian Grassmannian上广义共形结构的超扩展。额外发现是壳上的奇超对称生成元满足偶式Heisenberg交换关系，运动方程升级为对应osp(1|2n)超Weil表示的BGG型分解的微分算子复形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sato在1972年提出的通过无穷阶微分算子刻画Theta零值模形式性的方法虽被多位作者发展，但缺少统一自然的解释框架。作者旨在用超对称性为DOI提供天然来源，揭示其背后更深层的超群结构和物理意义。

Method: 作者通过识别超对称生成元的朴素指数为DOI，将Sato方法置于超对称框架下。具体地，利用超群OSp(1|2n)在Siegel平面超加厚上的作用来描述亏格n的Riemann theta函数，分析n=2时与三维N=1超共形群的对应关系，并建立壳上奇生成元满足的Heisenberg型交换关系，最终导出运动方程对应的BGG型微分算子复形。

Result: 得到了超对称对DOI的天然构造（奇超对称生成元的指数），揭示了Riemann theta函数与OSp(1|2n)超群的深层联系；在n=2情形精确还原了自由无质量标量超多重态（Laplace与Dirac方程的结合）；对一般n建立了超扩展的广义共形结构，并通过BGG型分解构造了对应的微分算子复形以及超Weil表示。

Conclusion: 超对称为Sato的DOI方法提供了统一且自然的解释框架，将数论中的模形式性问题与超群表示论（超Weil表示）和微分几何中的BGG型分解联系起来，开辟了Theta函数与超对称相互作用的交叉研究方向。

Abstract: In 1972, M. Sato proposed an approach to proving modularity of forms like Thetanullwerte by characterizing them via certain differential operators of infinite order (DOI) in the modular variable(s) alone. A DOI is an infinite series in derivatives decreasing so fast that it acts on holomorphic functions by a sheaf morphism. This approach was developed by several authors including Kashiwara, Kawai, Takei and Yoshida.
  We give an interpretation of this approach using supersymmetry which provides a natural source of DOIs: the naive exponential of any odd supersymmetry generator is a DOI. The case of the Riemann theta function of genus n is governed by the supergroup OSp(1|2n) (and its metaplectic cover) acting on a natural super-thickening of the Siegel plane. For n=2 this is the 3-dimensional N=1 superconformal group and the structure at hand is precisely the free massless scalar supermultiplet (combining the Laplace and Dirac equations). For n>2 we get a super-extension of the generalized conformal structure existing on the Lagrangian Grassmannian as on any Hermitian symmetric space.
  An additional interesting feature here is that the odd supersymmetry generators acting ``on-shell'' (i.e., in the space of solutions of the equations of motion) satisfy even-style Heisenberg commutation relations. These equations of motion upgrade to a complex of differential operators corresponding to a natural BGG-type resolution of the super-Weil representation of osp(1|2n).

</details>


### [88] [Algebraic cycles and Fano threefolds of genus 7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12950)
*Robert Laterveer*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究 genus 7 的一般素 Fano 三维簇 Y，在 Y×Y 上构造了一个 Abel-Jacobi 平凡但 Chow 群 A^4(Y×Y) 中非挠的显式 2-循环，从而证明 Y 不具有 Shen-Vial 意义下的乘法 Chow-Künneth 分解。同时证明任意 Fano 三维簇在代数等价意义下都具有乘法 Chow-Künneth 分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shen-Vial 提出的乘法 Chow-Künneth (mCK) 分解是研究代数簇 Chow 群结构的强大工具。此前对低 genus 的 Fano 三维簇已有相关研究，但 genus 7 的情况尚未明确。本文旨在探讨 genus 7 素 Fano 三维簇是否具有 mCK 分解，并进一步理解一般 Fano 三维簇在何种等价关系下具有该分解。

Method: 作者在 Y×Y 上显式构造了一个 2-循环（维数为 2，余维为 4，故属于 A^4(Y×Y)），通过 Abel-Jacobi 映射验证其平凡性，并利用 Chow 群的结构论证其非挠性。同时，利用代数等价模去分析 Fano 三维簇的 Chow-Künneth 分解性质。

Result: (1) 构造出 Y×Y 上的 Abel-Jacobi 平凡但非挠的 2-循环，从而 Y 不具有乘法 Chow-Künneth 分解。(2) 在代数等价模下，任意 Fano 三维簇均具有乘法 Chow-Künneth 分解。

Conclusion: genus 7 的一般素 Fano 三维簇是 Shen-Vial 意义下不具有乘法 Chow-Künneth 分解的又一例子，表明 mCK 分解的存在性是相当稀有的性质；但在较粗的代数等价意义下，mCK 分解对一切 Fano 三维簇成立，说明该分解的障碍本质上来自 Chow 群中 Abel-Jacobi 平凡但非挠的部分。

Abstract: Let $Y$ be a very general prime Fano threefold of genus 7. We exhibit an explicit 2-cycle on $Y\times Y$ that is Abel-Jacobi trivial but non-torsion in the Chow group $A^4(Y\times Y)$. As a consequence, $Y$ does not admit a multiplicative Chow-Künneth decomposition, in the sense of Shen-Vial. We also show that any Fano threefold has a multiplicative Chow-Künneth decomposition modulo algebraic equivalence.

</details>

Abstract: We tropically enumerate planar well-spaced elliptic curves with a fixed $j$-invariant. Combined with the recent genus 1 correspondence theorem of Cela--Koyama, this yields an alternative proof of Pandharipande's algebraic enumeration.

Conclusion: Lagrangian纤维化是构造扭曲超全纯层的有效工具，并通过这一途径验证了OG10型超Kähler流形上的两个重要猜想，为更广泛地解决超Kähler流形上的标准猜想提供了新思路。

Abstract: Following ideas of Kapustka-Kapustka, we use Lagrangian fibrations to construct twisted hyperholomorphic sheaves on products of hyperkähler manifolds of K3$^{[n]}$- and OG10-type. As applications, we prove the Lefschetz standard conjecture and the D-equivalence conjecture for hyperkähler manifolds of OG10-type.

</details>


### [91] [Sites and Grothendieck Topologies, Sites and Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13405)
*Arvid Siqveland*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提供了理解Grothendieck topos定义与应用所需的最小范畴论基础，阐述了site与sheaf的基本性质，并将其应用于模空间理论。文章证明了对具有特定性质的范畴C，可以构造C中对象的scheme范畴，该范畴是真范畴（proper category）而非如stack情形下的2-范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grothendieck topos是现代代数几何和范畴论中的核心概念，但其定义涉及较深的范畴论背景，对初学者构成障碍。此外，在stack理论中结构是2-范畴，这带来了额外的复杂性，作者希望找到一种构造方案，使得所得到的范畴是真范畴而非2-范畴，从而简化理论框架。

Method: 作者首先梳理并给出理解Grothendieck topos所需的最小范畴论工具，然后介绍site与sheaf的基本性质，并将这些工具应用于模空间理论。核心方法是对范畴C施加明确的（explicit）性质条件，在此条件下构造C中对象的scheme范畴，证明该构造得到一个proper category（而非stack情形下的2-范畴）。

Result: 文章证明了对满足特定条件的范畴C，可以构造一个scheme of objects的范畴，且该范畴是proper category，避免了stack理论中出现的2-范畴复杂性。同时，site和sheaf的理论工具成功应用于模空间理论。

Conclusion: 通过在特定条件下构造proper category而非2-范畴，本文为模空间理论及相关领域提供了一种更简洁的范畴论框架，降低了Grothendieck topos理论的应用门槛。

Abstract: We give the minimum of category theory necessary for understanding the definition and applications of Grothendieck topos. We state the basic properties of sites and sheaves, and give applications to the theory of moduli. We prove that for categories $\mathbf C$ with explicit stated properties, we can construct a category of schemes of objects in $\mathbf C$ which is a proper category rather than a $2$-category as in the case of stacks.

</details>

### [92] [Rigidity theorems for cone structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12907)

TL;DR: 本文研究微分流形上的锥结构（cone structures），特别是由常微分方程通过典范构造导出的锥结构。作者证明了：这类锥结构中，若某类等平凡（isotrivial）锥结构以n维射影空间中的一般曲线或三维射影空间中的直纹面为模型，则该锥结构必是平坦的（flat）。文章还讨论了该结果在四维因果几何和无色散Lax系统中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥结构是切丛中锥场的一般化对象，在微分几何、PDE理论和数学物理中有广泛应用。理解锥结构的局部不变量及何时是平坦的，是这一领域的核心问题之一。作者希望将等平凡锥结构的研究与由ODE典范构造得到的锥结构联系起来，并证明其在特定模型下的平坦性，从而为因果几何和无色散可积系统提供结构性的判定准则。

Method: 作者从微分流形上的锥结构定义出发，考虑由常微分方程通过典范（canonical）构造产生的锥结构。对这类锥结构，作者针对两类模型——n维射影空间中的一般曲线（generic curves）和三维射影空间中的直纹面（ruled surfaces）——建立等平凡性（isotriviality）条件，并证明满足该条件的锥结构必为平坦的。证明方法涉及射影几何、微分不变量以及锥结构的等价分类技术，并结合了ODE系统的几何化构造。

Result: 主要结果为：由常微分方程典范构造得到的锥结构，如果是等平凡的，且其模型为n维射影空间中的一般曲线，或三维射影空间中的直纹面，则该锥结构是平坦的。作者进一步将此结果应用于四维因果几何和无色散Lax系统的研究中，给出了相关结论。

Conclusion: 本文证明了ODE典范构造下等平凡锥结构在特定模型（一般曲线和直纹面）下的平坦性定理，为锥结构的分类提供了新的结果，并为四维因果几何和无色散Lax系统提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Cone structures on differentiable manifolds are fields of cones in the tangent bundle. A cone structure is isotrivial, modeled on a fixed immersed submanifold of the projective space, if at each point the projectivised cone is projectively equivalent to that submanifold. We consider cone structures arising from ordinary differential equations via a canonical construction. We prove that isotrivial cone structures in this class, modeled on generic curves in the $n$-dimensional projective space or ruled surfaces in the three-dimensional projective space, are flat. We also discuss applications to causal geometries in four dimensions and dispersionless Lax systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 每个非全脐超曲面都存在一个容许检验函数使其二阶变分为正，从而证明体积保持稳定性蕴含全脐性。该构造可推广至负内蕴曲率支撑情形，此时存在唯一的类时参数方向使得有限迹不再符号确定。

Abstract: We prove a rigidity theorem for compact spacelike capillary hypersurfaces in de~Sitter and Minkowski spaces: volume-preserving stability forces total umbilicity when the support is a spacelike totally umbilical hypersurface of nonnegative intrinsic curvature. Using the light-cone model, we construct conformal Killing fields tangent to the support and derive a unified Minkowski-type formula valid in all Lorentzian space forms. The resulting mean-zero functions satisfy an inhomogeneous Jacobi equation and the linearized capillary Robin boundary condition, and form canonical finite-dimensional test families. A finite-trace identity, supplemented in the de~Sitter cases by a nonpositive Dirichlet Green correction, detects the umbilicity defect \(n|h|^2-H^2\) with a definite sign; hence, every non-totally-umbilical hypersurface admits an admissible test function with positive second variation. The construction extends to supports of negative intrinsic curvature, where a unique timelike parameter direction prevents the finite trace from being sign-definite.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文将Chen-Zheng和Wang-Zheng的结果从平衡三维和四维情形推广到了任意复维数的平衡BTP情形，为非零常数Chern全纯截面曲率猜想在更广的流形类中提供了支持性证据。

Abstract: A well-known conjecture in complex geometry states that a compact Hermitian manifold with constant Chern holomorphic sectional curvature must be Kähler when the constant is nonzero and Chern flat when the constant is zero. The conjecture is known in complex dimension two and in several special classes in higher dimensions. For Hermitian metrics with Bismut-parallel torsion, the non-balanced case and the balanced threefold case were established by Chen--Zheng, while the balanced fourfold case was settled recently by Wang--Zheng. In this article, we prove the nonzero case for balanced Bismut-torsion-parallel Hermitian manifolds in every complex dimension. As a corollary, we confirm that a compact BTP Hermitian manifold with Chern holomorphic sectional curvature is a nonzero constant, then $g$ is Kähler.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在m=2的情形，对所有k≥3证明了Ξ_{k,2}的Morse指标为4k-3，零化度为3。在k=3的情形，对所有m≥1证明了存在阈值常数α*∈(1/3,1/2)，使得Morse指标为6m-4⌊mα*⌋-3；零化度通常为3，当mα*为整数时跳变为7。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了Karcher曲面两个子族Morse指标和零化度的显式公式，揭示了指标随参数k和m的线性增长规律，以及在特定参数值处零化度的跳跃现象，深化了对这类周期极小曲面稳定性结构的理解。

Abstract: For each integer $k \geq 3$ Hermann Karcher identified a complete singly periodic minimal surface $Ξ_k$ (unique up to similarity) with $2k$ ends asymptotic to the union of $k$ planes intersecting equiangularly along a single line and with genus zero in the quotient by a fundamental translation. Writing $Ξ_{k,m}$ for the quotient of $Ξ_k$ by translation through $m \geq 1$ fundamental periods, we study the Morse index and nullity of the subfamilies $Ξ_{k,2}$ and $Ξ_{3,m}$. In the $m=2$ case we prove for all $k \geq 3$ that $Ξ_{k,2}$ has Morse index $4k-3$ and nullity $3$. In the $k=3$ case we prove that there exists a real number $α^* \in (1/3,1/2)$ such that for all $m \geq 1$ the Morse index of $Ξ_{3,m}$ is $6m- 4 \lfloor mα^* \rfloor - 3$ and its nullity is $3$ unless $mα^*$ is an integer, in which case its nullity is $7$.

</details>

Result: 构造的示例中，无穷小Einstein形变空间实维数为8，其中可积方向构成实6维子空间，子空间外的所有方向均为受阻的（非可积），同时这些方向作为无穷小复形变也是非可积的。

Abstract: We construct a smooth canonically polarized threefold, not biholomorphic to a product of positive-dimensional varieties, whose normalized Kähler-Einstein metric admits a non-integrable infinitesimal Einstein deformation. The same tangent direction is non-integrable as an infinitesimal complex deformation. In fact, the space of infinitesimal Einstein deformations in our example has real dimension 8, its integrable directions form a real 6-dimensional subspace, and every direction outside that subspace is obstructed. This answers both parts of a suitably generalized version of a question posed by Dai, Wang, and Wei in real dimension 6.

</details>


### [97] [Heat kernel geometry and Gromov's volume growth conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13553)
*Jian Ge*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文肯定地回答了Gromov在1986年提出的一个几何问题：对于具有非负Ricci曲率且数量曲率至少为1的完备非紧Riemann流形，其体积增长满足Vol(B(p,R)) ≤ C_n R^{n-2}的最优上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gromov于1986年提出的这个猜想涉及完备非紧流形在非负Ricci曲率与数量曲率下界同时成立时的最优体积增长速率。这个问题将曲率条件与全局几何结构联系起来，是几何分析中的核心开放问题。

Method: 作者利用热核Fisher度量（heat-kernel Fisher metric）与Nash熵作为关键分析工具，通过这两者建立的精细几何分析框架来证明体积增长的最优上界。

Result: 证明了Gromov猜想的正确性，即当n维完备非紧Riemann流形满足非负Ricci曲率和数量曲率≥1时，任意球体积满足Vol(B(p,R)) ≤ C_n R^{n-2}，其中常数C_n仅依赖于维度n。

Conclusion: 该结果完整地解决了Gromov的猜想，表明非负Ricci曲率加上正数量曲率下界能显著限制流形的体积增长，使其从通常的R^n量级降到R^{n-2}量级。这揭示了曲率条件对全局几何结构的深刻约束。

Abstract: In 1986, Gromov asked whether every complete noncompact $n$-dimensional Riemannian manifold with nonnegative Ricci curvature and scalar curvature at least one satisfies:
  \[
  \Vol_g (B(p, R))\le C_{n}R^{n-2}
  \]
  for all $p\in M$ and $R>0$. We answer this question affirmatively using the heat-kernel Fisher metric and Nash entropy.

</details>
