### [98] [A Lapse in the Cosmological Constant Problem with Bulk Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12460)
*Justin Khoury,Benjamin Muntz,Antonio Padilla*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在之前提出的利用紧致额外维中各向异性标度解决宇宙学常数问题的基础上，将其推广到z=1形变（超出超局域极限），证明真空能抵消在背景方程层面仍然成立，但一般性外曲率耦合会引入额外标量鬼场；健康的二次谱要求Fierz-Pauli条件。作者提出一个基于五维爱因斯坦引力、拓扑形式通量和类空khoron标量场的协变实现，其中全局约束来自khoron叶空间上的辅助einbein，重整化物质真空能的常数平移从内禀爱因斯坦方程中代数抵消。最后，允许物质沿紧致维传播会产生有限的、拓扑敏感的Casimir贡献，需要附加谱条件来抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学常数问题（即物质真空能对引力场方程的辐射修正与观测到的宇宙学常数之间的巨大差异）是理论物理中最重要且未解决的难题之一。作者此前提出的基于紧致额外维中各向异性标度的框架在超局域（z=0）极限下能够使引力场方程对物质真空能的辐射修正不敏感，但该框架局限于此极限，缺乏沿紧致方向的动力学。本文旨在将这一机制推广到z=1形变，使其在保留真空能抵消特性的同时恢复紧致方向的动力学，并探索一个协变且可实现的物理构型。

Method: 作者在超局域z=0框架基础上引入z=1形变（恢复沿紧致方向动力学），通过分析背景方程中真空能抵消是否保持，以及微扰谱的稳定性条件，确定标量鬼场的消除要求Fierz-Pauli关系。随后提出一个具体实现：五维爱因斯坦引力耦合拓扑形式通量和一个类空khoron标量场（动态定义优选projectable叶状结构），其中全局约束源自khoron叶空间上的辅助einbein。还分析了允许物质在紧致维中传播时产生的Casimir贡献，并推导抑制这些贡献所需的谱条件。

Result: 真空能抵消在z=1形变后仍保持于背景方程层面；一般外曲率耦合会传播一个额外标量鬼场，而Fierz-Pauli关系可消除该鬼场。协变实现中，重整化物质真空能的常数平移从内禀Einstein方程中代数抵消。然而，物质沿紧致维传播会产生有限的、拓扑敏感的Casimir能量，该能量不会被机制自动移除，须附加额外的谱条件来抑制。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将真空能抵消机制从超局域极限推广到含动力学的z=1情形，并通过Fierz-Pauli条件获得健康的谱。虽然背景层面的真空能抵消依然有效且协变实现优雅，但Casimir贡献问题表明该机制并非完全自动地消除所有真空能效应，仍需要额外的谱条件约束，这为未来探索更完整的解决方案指明了方向。

Abstract: We have previously proposed a new approach to the cosmological constant problem based on anisotropic scaling in a compact extra dimension. In the ultra-local $z=0$ limit, the interplay between a projectable lapse, foliation-preserving diffeomorphisms, and higher-form fluxes renders the gravitational field equations insensitive to radiative corrections to the matter vacuum energy. Here we extend this framework beyond the ultra-local limit by introducing $z=1$ deformations that restore dynamics along the compact direction. We show that vacuum-energy cancellation persists at the level of the background equations, while generic extrinsic-curvature couplings propagate an additional scalar ghost. A healthy quadratic spectrum selects the Fierz-Pauli relation between these couplings. This motivates a particularly simple realization in terms of five-dimensional Einstein gravity, a top-form flux, and a spacelike khoron scalar field that dynamically defines a preferred projectable foliation. In this covariant formulation, the global constraint arises from an auxiliary einbein on the space of khoron leaves, and constant shifts of the renormalized matter vacuum energy cancel algebraically from the intrinsic Einstein equations. Finally, allowing matter to propagate around the compact dimension generates finite, topology-sensitive Casimir contributions that are not automatically removed by the mechanism. Suppressing these contributions requires additional spectral conditions on the propagating bulk degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [99] [Stabilizer complexity and the Python's lunch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12472)
*Abhirup Bhattacharya,Jatin Narde,Onkar Parrikar,Suprakash Paul*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了全息CFT中部分纠缠热（PET）态约化密度矩阵的稳定子复杂度，发现其Wigner负性由外部极值曲面面积与最小极值曲面面积之差决定，即exp[(A_out - A_min)/8G_N]，从而揭示了无Python's lunch时稳定子复杂度为O(1)，有Python's lunch时稳定子复杂度呈指数增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力/全息框架下量子电路复杂度与量子资源理论之间的联系，特别是通过稳定子复杂度（Wigner负性）来理解PET态约化密度矩阵的经典可模拟性，并揭示Python's lunch几何结构与计算复杂度之间的深层关联。

Method: 基于全息对偶框架，对带固定能量边界条件的PET态在子区域上的约化密度矩阵进行研究。利用CFT谱和重算子插入的伪随机性假设，计算约化密度矩阵相对于微正则密度矩阵的Wigner负性单调量，并将其与体几何中的外部极值曲面（outer extremal surface）和最小极值曲面（minimal extremal surface）的面积差建立联系。

Result: 发现PET态约化密度矩阵的Wigner负性正比于exp[(A_out - A_min)/8G_N]。在不存在Python's lunch的体几何中（A_out = A_min），稳定子复杂度为O(1)；而当存在Python's lunch时（A_out > A_min），稳定子复杂度获得指数级增强。

Conclusion: 稳定子复杂度可以作为一个操作上有意义的信息量来刻画全息态中"Python's lunch"结构的存在性——Python's lunch的出现会导致量子电路制备所需纠缠对应的经典模拟复杂度指数级增大，这为全息量子复杂度与量子资源理论提供了新的桥梁。

Abstract: In this note, we study the stabilizer complexity of the reduced density matrix corresponding to one side of a partially entangled thermal (PET) state with fixed energy boundary conditions in a holographic CFT. In particular, we study Wigner negativity, an operationally meaningful magic monotone which can be interpreted as the complexity of classically simulating any quantum circuit preparation of the reduced state on the subregion. Using assumptions on the pseudorandomness of the CFT spectrum and the heavy operator insertion, we observe that the Wigner negativity of the PET state relative to the microcanonical density matrix at the given energy is given by $\exp\left[\frac{1}{8G_N}(A_{\text{out}} - A_{\text{min}})\right]$, where $A_{\text{out}}$ is the area of the outer extremal surface, while $A_{\text{min}}$ is the area of the minimal extremal surface. Thus, the stabilizer complexity of the reduced density matrix on the boundary subregion is $O(1)$ in the absence of a python's lunch, but gets exponentially enhanced in the presence of a python's lunch in the bulk geometry.

</details>


### [100] [Constraints on the $O(n)$ model from a negative number of flavors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12481)
*Daniele Artico,Jasper Roosmale Nepveu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将O(n)模型中的味道数n视为变量，利用O(n)与Sp(-n)的对偶性将非微扰约束扩展到负偶数n，通过将一般味道结构的算符分解为不可约表示，揭示了不同算符标度维数中新的简并性，从而无需额外圈图计算即可从现有结果导出任意φ^k型算符的两圈反常维度，并在特定算符基下给出新的非重整化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O(n)模型中，将味道数n视为连续变量可产生算符谱的非微扰约束，但需要将这些关系显式化并推广到负偶数n，以揭示微扰理论中隐藏的更广泛结构，从而简化高阶计算并发现新的简并性与非重整化结果。

Method: 利用O(n)与Sp(-n)之间的对偶性，将味道数n延拓到负偶数；将一般味道结构的算符分解为不可约表示，结合微扰论分析揭示不同算符标度维数之间的简并性，并由此推导重整化群混合矩阵的模式与非重整化结果。

Result: 推导出任意φ^k型算符的两圈反常维度而无需额外圈图计算；在特定算符基下发现了新的非重整化结果；明确了重整化群混合矩阵的系统性模式。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅为微扰计算提供了高效工具，还可推广到算符乘积展开系数之间的关系，以及通过延长时空维度数产生的evanescence约束，展示了这些非微扰对称性结构在更广泛物理背景中的普适性。

Abstract: Treating the number of flavors $n$ as a variable in the $O(n)$ model leads to non-perturbative constraints on the spectrum of operators. Using the duality between $O(n)$ and $Sp(-n)$, we extend these relations to negative even values of $n$ and we make them explicit by decomposing operators with general flavor structure into irreducible representations. In perturbation theory, we exploit this structure to reveal novel degeneracies in the scaling dimensions of different operators, which persist for arbitrary $n$. This allows us to derive the two-loop anomalous dimension of any $φ^k$-type operator from existing results without additional loop calculations. The same mechanism dictates patterns in renormalization group mixing matrices, yielding new non-renormalization results in a specific operator basis. We comment on extending this framework to relations between operator product expansion coefficients and to analogous constraints arising from evanescence under continuation in the number of spacetime dimensions.

</details>


### [101] [Edge physics and the Casimir interaction in Maxwell--Chern--Simons theory on a strip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12622)
*Nicola Maggiore*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Symanzik边界量子场论框架下研究条形区域上的Maxwell–Chern–Simons理论，通过最一般的局部边界泛函推导出允许的边界条件，发现条带支持两个边界U(1)电流代数，其层级由Chern–Simons耦合和边界取向决定；有限相互作用能量可表示为闭式行列式（散射）形式，在纯Maxwell极限下产生长程幂律Casimir力，而在拓扑质量下产生Yukawa抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带边界量子场论中Maxwell–Chern–Simons系统的边界条件、边界电流代数结构与相互作用能量，理解拓扑效应与动力学效应在有限宽度几何中如何交织。

Method: 在Symanzik框架下，采用二次一阶切向导数截断，添加最一般的局部边界泛函，通过变分原理和规范对称性（边界Ward恒等式）推导可容许边界条件；利用散射/行列式表示求解有限相互作用能量。

Result: 条带支持两条边界U(1)电流代数，层级相反且由Chern–Simons耦合κ决定；边界速度由局部边界耦合控制，在翻转对称分支上等值反向。反射系数由同一组边界数据确定，相互作用能量具有闭式行列式表示：Maxwell极限下Casimir力为长程幂律，而Maxwell–Chern–Simons理论中拓扑质量m=κg²给出Yukawa级数在mh≫1时的指数抑制，并可分离拓扑边界数据与动力学体效应。

Conclusion: 该行列式形式提供了渐进区间的解析控制，清晰区分了拓扑边界数据（层级与异常流入）和动力学体效应（色散、边界混合与Casimir相互作用），为有边界Chern–Simons类理论提供了统一而精确的描述。

Abstract: We study Maxwell--Chern--Simons theories on a strip $\mathcal M=\mathbb R^{1,1}\times[0,h]$ within the Symanzik framework for quantum field theory with boundaries. We add the most general local boundary functional within the quadratic one-tangential-derivative truncation adopted throughout the paper and derive the admissible boundary conditions from locality and the variational principle, with gauge symmetry implemented through boundary Ward identities. The strip supports two boundary $U(1)$ current algebras, with opposite levels fixed by the Chern--Simons coupling $κ$ and by boundary orientation, while edge velocities depend on local boundary couplings. They are equal and opposite on the flip-symmetric branch, which is the natural choice for a spatially symmetric strip. The same boundary data determine the reflection coefficients for bulk modes and yield a closed determinant (scattering) representation of the finite interaction energy. In the Maxwell limit ($κ=0$) the inter-edge interaction is long-ranged and produces a power-law Casimir force, whereas in Maxwell--Chern--Simons theory the topological mass $m=κg^2$ generates Yukawa suppression at large separations $mh\gg1$. The determinant form also gives analytic control of the asymptotic regimes and separates topological boundary data (levels and anomaly inflow) from dynamical bulk effects (dispersion, boundary mixing and Casimir interaction).

</details>


### [102] [Type III von Neumann Algebras are Magical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12512)
*Mudassir Moosa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证了Type III von Neumann代数从根本上需要无限量的magic（非Clifford门资源）。通过考虑有限维量子系统（如格点系统）的热力学极限，证明如果热力学极限中的态只具有有界数量的magic，则其局部von Neumann代数不可能是Type III的。这对量子场论的量子模拟具有直接影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非Clifford门（即magic）是容错量子计算的关键资源，而Type III von Neumann代数是描述无限维量子系统（如量子场论和量子统计力学）的重要数学工具。探索两者之间的内在联系，特别是Type III代数结构对magic资源量的基本要求，对于理解量子场论的可模拟性至关重要。

Method: 从有限维量子系统（如格点系统）的热力学极限出发，分析其局部von Neumann代数的代数结构类型。通过考察态在该极限下所具有的magic（非Clifford门数量）是否有界，来判定对应的局部von Neumann代数是否可能为Type III类型，从而建立magic资源量与代数结构分类之间的理论联系。

Result: 证明了若热力学极限下的态仅具有有界数量的magic，则产生的局部von Neumann代数不可能是Type III类型的。由于量子场论中局部子区域的代数被广泛认为是Type III的，这意味着对量子场论进行量子模拟时，需要无限量的magic资源。

Conclusion: Type III von Neumann代数本质上需要无限量的magic资源。这一结果对量子场论的量子模拟提出了根本性的资源约束：由于局部子区域的代数结构为Type III，精确模拟量子场论需要无穷多的非Clifford门资源，这为评估量子计算在量子场论模拟中的可行性提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: The number of non-Clifford gates needed to perform a task, or simply \textit{magic}, is a resource for fault-tolerant quantum computation. Von Neumann algebras provide a formal mathematical structure to describe infinite-dimensional quantum systems, such as those in quantum field theory or quantum statistical mechanics. A particularly important class of von Neumann algebras is called Type III algebras, for which the standard notions of finite-dimensional systems such as density matrices and traces break down. In this work, we argue that Type III von Neumann algebras fundamentally require an infinite amount of magic. Specifically, we consider the thermodynamic limit of finite-dimensional quantum systems, such as lattice systems, and show that if the states in the thermodynamic limit possess only a bounded amount of magic, the resulting local von Neumann algebra cannot be of Type III. Our result has direct implications for the quantum simulations of quantum field theories, for which the algebra of a local subregion is known to be of Type III.

</details>


### [103] [D4-branes wrapped on topological disks from matter-coupled F(4) gauged supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12850)
*Patharadanai Nuchino,Parinya Karndumri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究物质耦合F(4)规范超引力中的超对称AdS_4×Σ解，其中Σ为具有非平凡U(1)全纯的拓扑圆盘或"半纺锤体"。在SO(3)×SO(3)规范群下耦合三个矢量多重态，构造了保持八个超荷并具有SO(2)×SO(2)或SO(2)_diag对称性的解，预期对偶于三维N=2 SCFT。部分解渐近于AdS_6几何，可视为五维N=2 SCFT中的余二维共形缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超对称AdS_4×Σ解及其全息对偶，特别是在物质耦合F(4)规范超引力框架下研究半纺锤体压缩产生的三维N=2 SCFT，并扩展已知解集发现新类别解。

Method: 在具有SO(3)×SO(3)规范群的F(4)规范超引力中耦合三个矢量多重态，在U(1)×U(1)子扇区内求解，随后提升到十维IIA型弦理论，解释为D4-D8膜系统缠绕在Σ上的构型。

Result: 得到两类解：一类渐近于局域AdS_6几何，对应五维N=2 SCFT中的余二维共形缺陷，其圆在AdS_6极限下解紧致化导致全息自由能发散；另一类解在三维SCFT对偶中具有有限自由能。这些结果既推广了已知解又提供了新类别解。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了半纺锤体上五维N=2 SCFT压缩到三维的理解，建立了新解与D4-D8膜系统的联系，既推广已有结果又发现新颖的解类别，为全息对偶研究提供了新的构造。

Abstract: We study a number of supersymmetric $AdS_4\times Σ$ solutions with $Σ$ being a topological disk with non-trivial $U(1)$ holonomy on the boundary or a ``half-spindle'' from matter-coupled $F(4)$ gauged supergravity. The gauged supergravity is coupled to three vector multiplets with $SO(3)\times SO(3)$ gauge group. The resulting solutions preserve eight supercharges and $SO(2)\times SO(2)$ or $SO(2)_{\text{diag}}$ symmetries and are expected to be dual to $N=2$ SCFTs in three dimensions arising from compactifications of five-dimensional $N=2$ SCFT on a half-spindle. All of the solutions lie within the $U(1)\times U(1)$ subsector of the matter-coupled $F(4)$ gauged supergravity that can be embedded in massive type IIA theory. After uplifted to ten dimensions, the solutions can be interpreted as a system of D4-D8-branes wrapped on $Σ$. Some of the solutions are asymptotic to a locally $AdS_6$ geometry and can be identified as codimension-2 conformal defects within the $N=2$ SCFT in five dimensions. For these solutions, the circle inside $Σ$ decompactifies in the $AdS_6$ limit rendering the holographic free energy infinite. There also exist solutions with finite free energy in the dual three-dimensional SCFTs. In addition, we show that the results both extend the previously known solutions and provide a novel class of solutions.

</details>


### [104] [Limits of the inverse scattering problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13105)
*Matvei Fedin,Kirill Gubarev,Andrey Morozov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将传统X射线/Radon层析成像（从线积分重建密度函数）推广到粒子情形的问题。关键约束是粒子速度必须有限，否则问题退化为经典情形。文章通过简单理论示例和机器学习管道来恢复势函数，研究最小可行速度如何依赖于势函数参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统层析成像基于X射线沿直线传播的假设，但当研究对象是粒子时，粒子受势场影响会产生弯曲轨迹，经典的Radon变换不再直接适用。作者希望将层析成像推广到粒子层面，并理解在什么条件下（特别是粒子速度低到什么程度）这种推广仍然可行。

Method: 论文采用两种互补的方法：(1) 构建简单理论示例来分析问题；(2) 使用机器学习管道（pipeline）来从粒子测量的线积分数据中恢复势函数，从而实证评估在给定速度下势函数重建的可行性。

Result: 论文获得了最小可行粒子速度随势函数参数变化的规律，并通过机器学习方法验证了在该速度下限条件下势函数可以成功恢复。

Conclusion: 粒子层析成像是可行但有条件的：粒子速度存在一个由势函数参数决定的下限，低于该速度时势函数无法被唯一恢复；机器学习方法为这类广义逆问题提供了有效的求解途径。

Abstract: The main goal of tomography is the reconstruction of density function out of its line integrals (integral measurements of this density along x-ray lines). Such construction is possible and known as inverse x-ray/Radon transformations. We are interested in generalization of this problem to the case of particles. For this we need to limit the speed of these particles, otherwise the problem is reduced to the previous one. The question we discuss in this paper is how low can this speed be depending on the parameters of the studied potential. We study this problem using simple theoretical examples and Machine Learning pipeline to restore the potential.

</details>


### [105] [Exact Evaluation of Lattice-Regularized Scalar Field Vacuum Amplitude via Site Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13117)
*Vadim Asnin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法，用于在任意维数晶格上计算具有任意势能的单标量场的真空振幅，该方法通过对路径积分中动能指数因子进行所有格点置换的对称化，并对对称化后的表达式进行Waring分解来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的晶格上标量场真空振幅计算可能面临计算复杂度高或方法适用范围受限的问题，本文旨在提出一种全新的、适用于任意维数和任意势能的计算方案。

Method: 该方法包含两个关键步骤：首先，将路径积分中的动能指数因子在所有晶格位点的排列下进行对称化处理；其次，对对称化后的表达式应用Waring分解（将对称多项式表示为幂和的线性组合），从而简化真空振幅的计算。

Result: 提出了一种可用于任意维数、任意势能的单标量场晶格真空振幅的计算新方案，但摘要中未给出具体数值结果或验证。

Conclusion: 通过对称化与Waring分解相结合的策略，为晶格上标量场真空振幅的计算提供了一种新的、具有通用性的理论框架。

Abstract: A novel approach to a computation of vacuum amplitude for a single scalar field in any number of dimensions with arbitrary potential on a lattice is proposed. The computation involves symmetrization of kinetic exponential factor in the path integral over all permutations of lattice sites and Waring decomposition of the symmetrized expression.

</details>


### [106] [Single-Variable Solutions in Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13155)
*W. A. Sabra,R. Slim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了四族依赖于单变量的时空度量，适用于一大类耦合标量场和阿贝尔规范场的D维引力理论，并将其应用于无规范N=2、D=4超引力、N=8超引力的一致截断以及标量流形为对称陪集SL(N,R)/SO(N,R)的理论，得到具有非平凡标量和规范场构型的显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一套统一的广义形式体系，用于构造多种引力理论中依赖于单变量的时空解，从而揭示标量流形几何在解的结构中所起的关键作用。

Method: 通过构造四族依赖于单变量的时空度量通用形式体系，将其适用于D维引力理论（耦合标量和Abelian规范场），并具体应用于无规范N=2超引力、N=8超引力一致截断、以及对称陪集流形SL(N,R)/SO(N,R)标量场理论。

Result: 成功推导出无规范N=2、D=4超引力耦合矢量多重态的依赖于单变量的解，获得了N=8超引力一致截断的显式解，以及标量场参数化对称陪集流形SL(N,R)/SO(N,R)的理论解。所有情况下标量流形的几何均决定了具有非平凡标量和规范场构型的解的结构。

Conclusion: 标量流形的几何结构在引力理论解的构造中起着核心作用，所列四族单变量解为理解超引力理论中规范场与标量场的非平凡耦合提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We construct four families of spacetime metrics depending on a single variable for a broad class of $D$-dimensional gravitational theories coupled to scalar and Abelian gauge fields. As applications of the general formalism, we derive one-variable solutions of ungauged $\mathcal{N}=2$, $D=4$ supergravity coupled to vector multiplets. We also obtain explicit solutions for a consistent truncation of $\mathcal{N}=8$, $D=4$ supergravity, as well as for theories whose scalar fields parametrize the symmetric coset manifolds $SL(N,\mathbb{R})/SO(N,\mathbb{R})$. In all cases, the geometry of the scalar manifold plays a central role in determining the structure of the resulting solutions with nontrivial scalar and gauge field configurations.

</details>


### [107] [Lee-Yang paradigm of phase transition in eigenstate thermalized systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13174)
*Yongjiang Xu,Weixin Sun,Chushun Tian,Huajia Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文展示了在全息共形场论中，满足本征态热化的系统可以出现属于Lee-Yang范式的热力学类相变。通过将自关联函数的本征态期望映射为虚拟相互作用瞬子气体的配分函数，作者发现当中心荷c趋于无穷时，配分函数的一对复共轭零点移动到实数轴上的禁戒奇点处，系统跨越该奇点从低瞬子逸度相变到高瞬子逸度相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立量子系统中的相变现象长期以来难以实现和理解，作者希望探索在本征态热化系统中是否能够出现类热力学相变，并揭示其背后的物理机制和普适范式。

Method: 作者在全息共形场论的框架下，将本征态自关联函数的期望值映射为虚拟相互作用瞬子气体的配分函数Z_gauge(z)，利用虚时间的共形映射z=1-e^{-τ}，将中心荷c作为瞬子逸度与体积的模拟参数。通过分析配分函数零点的分布和自由能的标度行为，研究相变的发生机制。

Result: 研究发现，当c→∞时，配分函数Z_gauge(z)的一对复共轭零点移至实数轴著名的禁戒奇点处，系统跨越该奇点时发生从低逸度相到高逸度相的转变，伴随自由能标度行为和主导微观构型的显著变化，这与Lee-Yang相变范式完全一致。

Conclusion: 本征态热化系统可以展现出丰富的相变物理，Lee-Yang范式同样适用于此类系统的相变描述，这为理解孤立量子系统中的相变现象提供了新的理论框架和视角。

Abstract: As phase transitions in isolated quantum systems remain elusive, here we show how a thermodynamic-like phase transition, falling into the Lee-Yang paradigm, can arise in systems displaying eigenstate thermalization. Specifically, we show that in holographic conformal field theories, the eigenstate expectation of the auto-correlation function can be mapped to the partition function ${\cal Z}_{gauge}(z)$ of a virtual interacting instanton gas, with the conformal mapping of the imaginary time: $z=1-e^{-τ}$ and the central charge $c$ mimicking the instanton fugacity and volume, respectively. We find that akin to the Lee-Yang paradigm, for $c\to\infty$ a pair of complex conjugate zeros of ${\cal Z}_{gauge}(z)$ move to the real axis located at the famous forbidden singularity. Passing through the singularity the system transits from the low- to high-fugacity phase, accompanied by dramatic changes in scaling behaviors of the free energy and dominant microscopic configurations. Our findings indicate that physics of phase transitions from eigenstate thermalization is very rich.

</details>


### [108] [Phase transition from eigenstate thermalization: forbidden singularity and instanton proliferation via AGT correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13246)
*Yongjiang Xu,Weixin Sun,Chushun Tian,Huajia Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在二维共形场论中建立了一个重要对应关系：本征态自关联函数中的"禁区奇点"（不满足本征态热化特征的关键签名）对应着本征态系统中的一个相变。通过AGT对偶，作者将自关联函数映射到N=2超对称规范理论的Nekrasov配分函数（描述瞬子气体），并在重-轻极限的对应下发现瞬子气体出现两种相——由串状Young图构型主导的相，类似禁闭相与退禁闭相。当瞬子从一侧增殖时发生相变，其行为模仿Lee-Yang理论，临界逸度恰好对应禁区奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在理论物理中，将不同领域的问题建立联系往往能推动突破性进展。本文旨在将2D CFT中本征态自关联函数的"禁区奇点"现象（本征态热化ETH的关键签名）与相变现象建立对应关系，从而从一个更深刻的视角理解该现象的物理本质。

Method: 作者利用AGT对偶，将本征态自关联函数与N=2 SUSY规范理论的Nekrasov配分函数联系起来（后者描述瞬子气体系统）。通过取重-轻极限的对应方，他们在瞬子气体中找到两个相，由不同结构的串状Young图构型主导。通过分析这些构型的热力学性质，并借鉴Lee-Yang理论框架，推导出临界逸度。

Result: 发现瞬子气体存在两个不同的相，分别由具有不同结构和热力学性质的串状Young表构型主导，类似于禁闭相与退禁闭相的分离。相变发生在瞬子增殖的一侧，临界逸度被精确计算出来，且恰好对应CFT中自关联函数的禁区奇点位置。

Conclusion: 禁区奇点在本征态热化中并非特殊现象，而是对应着相关瞬子气体模型中的热力学相变。这一发现建立了ETH签名与相变理论之间出人意料的联系，为理解本征态热化提供了新的物理视角，也展示了AGT对偶在连接量子场论与统计力学之间的强大作用。

Abstract: In theoretical physics, finding connections between problems that appear in distinct contexts is an important way to leapfrog progresses, often by illuminating deep aspects that may otherwise seem obscure. In this paper, we consider in 2d CFTs the phenomenon of forbidden singularities in auto-correlation functions -- a key signature of eigenstate thermalization. We show that they correspond to phase transitions in the context of eigenstates. The connection is made explicit by utilizing the AGT correspondence, which relates eigenstate auto-correlations to the Nekrasov partition functions describing an instanton gas of the $\mathcal{N}=2$ SUSY gauge theories. We show that by taking the counter-part of the heavy-light limit, two phases emerge for the instanton gas. They are dominated by configurations represented by string-like Young tableaux with distinct structures and thermodynamic properties, which bare resemblance to the confined and the deconfined phases. A phase transition occurs as instantons proliferate from one side, in a manner that mimics the Lee-Yang theory. We work out the critical fugacity and find it corresponding exactly to the forbidden singularity.

</details>


### [109] [3d $\mathcal{N}=$ 4 rank-0 SCFT from punctured lens space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13300)
*Sungjoon Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文从一对平行M5膜在带单顶点移除的透镜空间L(2n+3,2)上的紧化出发，推导出Gang-Kim-Stubbs理论T_n（即M(2n+3,2) Virasoro最小模型的3d N=4 rank-0超共形场论的拓扑A-twist描述），并由此提出由带刺孔的透镜空间L(2n+3,1)紧化产生的一族3d N=2阿贝尔规范理论，其红外相位实现了M(2n+3,2)模张量范畴的Galois轨道中的酉成员，同时基于透镜空间的双手性条件推测了自镜像rank-0不动点的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Gang-Kim-Stubbs理论（3d N=4 rank-0 SCFT）置于更广泛的M5-brane紧化几何框架中理解，并推广到带刺孔透镜空间紧化情形，以构造实现Virasoro最小模型模张量范畴Galois轨道中其他成员的理论。

Method: 利用M5-brane在带单顶点移除的透镜空间L(2n+3,2)上的紧化推导Gang-Kim-Stubbs理论；推广到带刺孔的L(2n+3,1)透镜空间，提出对应的3d N=2阿贝尔规范理论族；利用透镜空间的双手性（amphichirality）条件推测自镜像rank-0不动点。

Result: 提出了一族源自带刺孔透镜空间L(2n+3,1)的3d N=2阿贝尔规范理论，其红外相位实现M(2n+3,2)模张量范畴的Galois轨道中酉成员；并基于透镜空间双手性条件推测了自镜像rank-0不动点的存在。

Conclusion: 该工作为Virasoro最小模形的3d实现提供了统一的M5-brane几何视角，并猜测存在新的自镜像rank-0超共形不动点理论。

Abstract: {\it Gang-Kim-Stubbs} theory $\mathcal{T}_n$ --- a pioneering 3d bulk description of $M(2n+3,2)$ Virasoro minimal model as $\mathcal{N}=4$ rank-0 superconformal field theory upon topological A-twist --- is derived from the compactification of a pair of parallel M5-branes on {\it lens space} $L(2n+3,2)$ with a single vertex removed. From this perspective, we propose a family of 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ abelian gauge theories arising from the punctured $L(2n+3,1)$ lens space whose infrared phases realize the unitary member in the Galois orbit of $M(2n+3,2)$ modular tensor category. We also conjecture self-mirror rank-0 fixed points from amphichirality condition of the lens space.

</details>


### [110] [Frame-dependency of the confinement temperature in a strongly-coupled plasma under rotation: a holographic description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13301)
*Nelson R. F. Braga,Alexsandre L. Ferreira*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过最一般的Myers-Perry黑洞解推广了先前关于旋转QGP中临界温度参考系依赖性的全息结果，发现打破球对称性后，共转观测者测量的局域温度具有非平凡的角依赖性，随旋转速度可增、可减或非单调变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现格点QCD计算与全息模型在旋转夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）效应上的分歧源于参考系选择不同：静态观测者测得禁闭温度随旋转降低，而共转观测者测得相反趋势。理解临界温度的参考系依赖性对描述旋转QGP至关重要，但此前研究仅考虑了等角动量（球对称）情形。

Method: 将等离子体描述为最一般的Myers-Perry黑洞解，打破等角动量情形下的球对称性，从而在更一般的框架下分析共转观测者所测量的局域温度及其角依赖性。

Result: 在打破球对称性后，共转观测者测量的局域温度呈现非平凡的角向依赖：随着旋转速度增加，温度可以降低、升高甚至呈现非单调行为。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，在更一般的旋转几何（非球对称）下，旋转QGP的临界温度行为比此前球对称情形更为丰富和复杂，进一步证实了临界温度的参考系依赖性，为全面理解旋转QGP的热力学性质提供了更完整的全息框架。

Abstract: Recently, it was demonstrated that the disagreement between lattice calculations and holographic models, regarding rotational effects in the quark-gluon plasma (QGP), occurs due to different choices of reference frames. While a static observer measures a confinement temperature that decreases with rotation, one in a co-rotating frame finds the opposite behavior. Both results are correct, and a comprehension of the frame-dependency of the critical temperature is a significant step in the description of the QGP under rotation. In this article, we generalize this previous holographic result by describing the plasma through the most general Myers-Perry black hole solution. This breaks the spherical symmetry present in the equal angular momentum case, considered before. As a consequence, the local temperature measured by a co-rotating observer has a non-trivial angular dependence: it can decrease, increase or even behave non-monotonically when rotational velocity increases.

</details>


### [111] [False-vacuum bubbles in sphaleron scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11370)
*Manuel A. Martínez Sánchez,Christoph Adam,Danial Saadatmand*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了(1+1)维具有假真空的对称变形φ⁶标量场论中两个亮sphaleron的碰撞动力学。通过考虑"势垒"和"势阱"两种单参数模型，系统研究了不同初始速度和变形参数下的对心碰撞，发现丰富的散射末态，包括kink-antikink对产生、真假真空中的长寿命振荡子、假真空区中多个振荡子的传播以及辐射衰变。特别值得注意的是发现了由kink-antikink对围成的长寿命假真空泡，反复坍缩和膨胀后才最终衰变为振荡子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索具有假真空的变形φ⁶场论中sphaleron碰撞的动力学行为，此前这类系统的sphaleron散射动力学研究尚不充分，理解不稳定局域化构型在非线性场论中的作用具有重要理论意义。

Method: 采用两种单参数实现（势垒模型和势阱模型）描述对称变形φ⁶理论，首先回顾静态和线性不稳定性性质，然后对增强的sphaleron进行大范围初始速度和变形参数下的对心碰撞数值模拟。

Result: 发现丰富的碰撞动力学末态：kink-antikink对产生、真假真空中长寿命振荡子的生成、假真空区域中多个振荡子的传播以及辐射衰变。最显著的发现是由kink-antikink对包围的长寿命假真空泡，它周期性地坍缩和再膨胀，最终衰变为振荡子。

Conclusion: 具有假真空的变形φ⁶理论展现出比以前已知更为丰富的sphaleron动力学，为理解不稳定局域化构型在非线性场论中的作用提供了新的见解。

Abstract: We investigate the collision dynamics of two bright sphalerons in a (1+1)-dimensional deformed $φ^6$ scalar field theory with a symmetric potential possessing false vacua. Two one-parameter realizations of the model, referred to as the barrier and well models, are considered and their static and linear instability properties are first reviewed. We then study head-on collisions of boosted sphalerons over a broad range of initial velocities and deformation parameters. The scattering dynamics exhibit a rich variety of final states, including the production of kink-antikink pairs, long-lived oscillons in true and false vacuum, multiple oscillons propagating in false-vacuum regions, and radiative decay. A particularly remarkable outcome is the emergence of a long-lived bubble of the false broken vacuum bounded by a kink-antikink pair, which repeatedly collapses and re-expands before eventually decaying into an oscillon. These results demonstrate that deformed $φ^6$ theories with false vacua exhibit considerably richer sphaleron dynamics than previously known and provide new insight into the role of unstable localized configurations in nonlinear field theories.

</details>


### [112] [Holographic Renormalization for String-Derived Lovelock--Horndeski Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13319)
*Tianhao Wu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文针对五维弦引导Lovelock–Horndeski（SDLH）理论，在其精确线性胀子渐近局部AdS分支上，构造了任意边界度规和时空相关标量源的重整化生成泛函。作者建立了边界协变径向递推框架，统一处理变分问题、局域反作用、对数阻碍、单点函数和Ward恒等式，并证明了在正则分支上四个标量归一化不变的全息组合可由连续约化耦合全局有理反演，从而展示全息响应可作为弦约化的过定边界指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦引导的高曲率标量–张量引力将微观耦合数据编码在边界响应中，核心问题在于全息观测量能否重构底层高维参数。作者对此提出明确回答，旨在建立从边界全息数据到弦约化参数的显式重构映射。

Method: 采用AdS/CFT对应中的holographic renormalization方法：对SDLH理论构造重整化生成泛函，引入边界协变径向递推层级统一变分问题与局域反作用，用两个响应行列式组织递归和共振阻碍，分析Weyl反常的欧拉密度、Weyl平方密度及曲率–标量平方形式，并在正则分支上寻找全息组合对约化耦合的全局有理逆映射。

Result: 在正则分支上，四个标量归一化不变的全息组合实现为连续约化耦合的全局有理反函数；固定紧致化维数时映射秩最大；曲率反常和可无符号歧义地重构高维Gauss–Bonnet系数。两项响应行列式统一了递归、共振阻碍及度规–标量混合。

Conclusion: 全息响应提供了弦约化的显式、过定边界指纹，证明全息可观测量能够重构底层高维耦合参数，且重构具有唯一性和可计算性。

Abstract: String-derived higher-curvature scalar--tensor gravities encode microscopic coupling data in boundary response, raising the question of whether holographic observables can reconstruct the underlying higher-dimensional parameters. We answer this question for the five-dimensional string-derived Lovelock--Horndeski (SDLH) theory on its exact linear-dilaton asymptotically locally AdS branch, constructing the renormalized generating functional for an arbitrary boundary metric and spacetime-dependent scalar source. A boundary-covariant radial hierarchy unifies the variational problem, local backreaction, logarithmic obstruction, finite one-point functions, and Ward identities. Two response determinants organize the recursion, resonant obstructions, and metric--scalar mixing. The Weyl anomaly condenses into an Euler density, a Weyl-squared density, and a single curvature--scalar square whose paired variations generate the metric and scalar obstructions. The resulting renormalized functional carries string-selected coupling data into boundary geometry, operator response, anomaly coefficients, and a calculable interface with gravitational observables. On the regular branch, four scalar-normalization-invariant holographic combinations admit a global rational inverse to the continuous reduced couplings. At fixed compactification dimension the map has maximal rank, while the curvature-anomaly sum reconstructs the higher-dimensional Gauss--Bonnet coefficient without sign ambiguity. Holographic response thus provides an explicit, overdetermined boundary fingerprint of the underlying string reduction.

</details>


### [113] [Localised Horizons and Holographic Thermodynamics: Supercooling in the 1/D Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13557)
*Prateek Agrawal,Gaurang Ramakant Kane,Vazha Loladze*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过在D+1维爱因斯坦-标量引力中对偶非共形规范理论，使用1/D展开揭示了热禁闭相变中普适、与标量势细节无关的预测结构，并发现最大过冷度在领头阶由去禁闭相中的声速决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全息框架中，四维禁闭规范理论通常通过选择特定标量势的五维爱因斯坦-标量引力来建模，但这些预测往往依赖于势的具体形式。作者希望找到不依赖势的细节、具有普适性的预测结构，从而更深刻地理解热禁闭相变。

Method: 作者将引力对偶推广到D+1维，并采用1/D展开方法。在这种展开下，去禁闭相的黑膜几何由于效应局域化在视界附近而可以在领头阶解析构造。通过分析解的存在性条件（最低温度T_min）推导出最大过冷度的表达式。

Result: 发现该解在低于最小温度T_min时不存在，最大过冷度ε_sc=1-T_min/T_c通常被1/D^2因子抑制。在领头阶，最大过冷度由临界温度下规范理论去禁闭相中的声速决定，即ε_sc=c_s^2(T_c)/2。这些预测在指数超势、改进全息以及球面上N=4超杨-米尔斯的热相变中均得到验证。

Conclusion: 研究表明热禁闭相变中最大过冷度具有普适性，不依赖于标量势的具体细节，其值由去禁闭相中的声速确定，这暗示了非共形规范理论热力学性质中更深层的普适结构。

Abstract: In holography, four-dimensional confining gauge theories are often modelled by five-dimensional Einstein--scalar gravity by choosing a specific form of the scalar potential. In a large class of non-conformal theories, we show that a predictive structure emerges for the thermal confinement transition by generalising the gravitational dual to $D+1$ dimensions and using a $1/D$ expansion. These results are independent of the details of the scalar potential, hinting towards universality. The black brane geometry dual to the deconfined phase can be analytically constructed due to its effects being localised near the horizon at leading order. The solution does not exist below a minimal temperature $T_{\rm min}$ and the maximum possible supercooling in the transition $ε_{\rm sc} = 1-T_{\rm min}/T_{\rm c}$ is generically suppressed by a factor of $1/D^2$. Remarkably, the maximum supercooling at the leading order is set by the speed of sound in the deconfined phase of the gauge theory at the critical temperature, $ε_{\rm sc}=c_s^2(T_{\rm c})/2$. These predictions agree with explicit calculations in an exponential superpotential, improved holography, and the thermal transition in $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills on a sphere.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Dynamical uncertainty geometry for nonlinear transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12521)
*Giacomo Tommei*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出动力不确定性几何（DUG）框架，将后验不确定性、未来观测和模型诱导传输三个经常混淆的要素分离，通过质量排序的可信过滤、未来实验的概率律与Fisher几何描述，以及带标签的传输骨架，在共同包围概率坐标下追踪物理上有意义的类别。论文建立了质量索引持续模块的稳定性结果，并在短弧轨道确定和地月平面圆型限制性三体问题两个基准问题上验证了框架的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力系统中的不确定性常常由后验几何本身无法显现的传输结构所组织。现有的方法通常将后验不确定性、未来观测和模型诱导的传输三者混淆在一起，且只关注单一阈值下的可信集，无法追踪跨可信度水平的物理类别。

Method: DUG框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 质量排序的可信过滤（mass-ranked credible filtration），用于索引后验可信集；(2) 通过诱导概率律和Fisher几何描述未来实验；(3) 带标签的传输骨架（labelled transport skeleton），表示动力学上不同的结果。框架还建立了质量索引持续模块的稳定性结果，涵盖连续、精化和有限原子三种公式，支持从数值近似和加权网格中量化控制持久性计算。

Result: 在两个基准问题中验证：短弧轨道确定场景下，DUG在连通的不确定性区域内识别出动力学上不同的返回类别，并揭示了基于信息与基于局部Fisher观测设计准则之间的差异；在地月平面圆型限制性三体问题中，DUG揭示了连通可信区域内共存的多重首次命中传输结果，并提供诊断工具评估其数值分辨率。

Conclusion: 将后验几何的拓扑摘要与传输标签和未来实验相结合，能够比单独使用后验概率或动力学分类提供更丰富的不确定性描述。

Abstract: Uncertainty in nonlinear dynamical systems is often organized by transport structures that are not apparent from posterior geometry alone. We introduce Dynamical Uncertainty Geometry (DUG), a framework that separates three ingredients that are frequently conflated: posterior uncertainty, future observations, and model-induced transport. DUG indexes posterior credible sets and transport-conditioned subsets by a common enclosed-probability coordinate, allowing physically meaningful classes to be tracked across credibility levels rather than examined at a single threshold. The framework combines a mass-ranked credible filtration, a description of future experiments through their induced probability laws and Fisher geometry, and a labelled transport skeleton representing dynamically distinct outcomes. We establish stability results for the resulting mass-indexed persistence modules under simultaneous perturbations of posterior density and probability measure, including continuum, refinement, and finite atomic formulations. These results provide quantitative control of persistence computed from numerical approximations and weighted grids. Two benchmark problems illustrate the framework. In a short-arc orbit-determination setting, DUG identifies dynamically distinct return classes within a connected uncertainty region and highlights a difference between information-based and local Fisher-based observation-design criteria. In the Earth-Moon planar circular restricted three-body problem, DUG reveals multiple first-hit transport outcomes coexisting within a connected credible region and provides diagnostics for assessing their numerical resolution. Together, these examples show how topological summaries of posterior geometry, when coupled to transport labels and future experiments, yield a richer description of uncertainty than either posterior probabilities or dynamical classifications alone.

</details>


### [115] [Neveu--Schwarz Irregular Vertex Operators, Decomposition Theorems, and Bilinear Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12727)
*Hajime Nagoya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为Neveu–Schwarz代数构造了秩零不规则顶点算子，证明了其存在性与唯一性，并建立了不规则情形下的分解定理，最终推导出与量子Painlevé V和IV tau函数方程一致的双线性微分算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将顶点代数与超共形场论中的分解定理推广到不规则情形，并建立不规则顶点算子与量子Painlevé方程之间的深层联系，弥合共形场论与可积系统之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 构造秩零不规则顶点算子作为同秩不规则Verma模之间的线性映射，满足超共形对易关系和渐近条件；2) 在非退化假设Λ₂ₚ≠0下证明存在唯一性；3) 将自由费米子Fock模与秩p不规则Verma模的张量积分解为两个Virasoro不规则Verma模的无穷直和；4) 利用配对和模插入推导双线性微分方程。

Result: 获得了不规则顶点算子的存在唯一性定理，证明了不规则分解定理及其兼容的算子分解，导出的双线性微分算子经过参数识别和规范变换后与量子Painlevé V和IV tau函数方程中的算子完全一致。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个统一的框架，将Neveu–Schwarz代数的超共形场论工具与量子Painlevé方程联系起来，为研究不规则共形块和可积系统提供了新的途径。

Abstract: We construct rank-zero irregular vertex operators for the Neveu--Schwarz algebra as linear maps between irregular Verma modules of the same rank satisfying the usual superconformal commutation relations and an irregular asymptotic condition. Under the nondegeneracy assumption $Λ_{2p}\neq0$, we prove their existence and uniqueness. We then extend the decomposition theorem for the Neveu--Schwarz algebra to the irregular setting: the tensor product of the free-fermion Fock module with a rank-$p$ Neveu--Schwarz irregular Verma module decomposes into an infinite direct sum of tensor products of two Virasoro irregular Verma modules. We also prove a compatible decomposition of the irregular vertex operators into tensor products of Virasoro irregular vertex operators. Using suitable pairings and mode insertions, we derive bilinear differential equations for weighted sums of products of Virasoro irregular conformal blocks of types $(0,0,1)$ and $(0,2)$. After explicit parameter identifications and a gauge transformation in the Painlevé V case, the resulting bilinear differential operators agree with those appearing in the quantum Painlevé V and IV tau-function equations.

</details>


### [116] [From Lie--Rinehart Algebras to $F$-Manifold Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12802)
*Yufeng Pei,Yunhe Sheng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为每个Lie–Rinehart代数在其基代数与模的直和上构造了一个F-流形代数，指出这与Loday–Vallette著作中的断言相反，所得结构通常不是Poisson的，并研究了正次数理想幂次何时为Poisson理想，以及Leibnizator与Lie–Rinehart微分之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在澄清并修正已有文献（Loday–Vallette）中关于Lie–Rinehart代数与Poisson结构关系的错误断言，建立F-流形代数与Lie–Rinehart代数之间的正确联系。

Method: 在基代数与模的直和上构造F-流形代数结构；通过分析对称Poisson代数中正次数理想的幂次是否为Poisson理想来判定结构性质；利用Leibnizator与Lie–Rinehart微分的关联，以及有限投射常秩模上Leibnizator的迹来恢复anchor映射。

Result: 证明所得F-流形代数一般不是Poisson的（纠正了Loday–Vallette的断言）；给出了正次数理想幂次为Poisson理想的条件；建立了Leibnizator与Lie–Rinehart微分的关系；对有限投射常秩模，Leibnizator的迹可恢复anchor并得到单射anchor的刚性结果。

Conclusion: 通过代数与几何例子验证了结果，为Lie–Rinehart代数与F-流形结构之间建立了更精确的理论框架，并提供了新的不变量（Leibnizator的迹）用于研究anchor的性质。

Abstract: For every Lie--Rinehart algebra, we construct an $F$-manifold algebra on the
  direct sum of its base algebra and module. Contrary to the assertion in
  \cite[Proposition 13.3.26]{LodayVallette}, the resulting structure is generally
  not Poisson. We determine when powers of the positive-degree ideal in the
  associated symmetric Poisson algebra are Poisson ideals, and relate the
  Leibnizator to the Lie--Rinehart differential. For a finite projective module
  of constant rank, the trace of the Leibnizator recovers the anchor and yields a
  rigidity result for injective anchors. We conclude with algebraic and
  geometric examples.

</details>


### [117] [A discrete Smorodinsky--Winternitz I superintegrable system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12899)
*Vutha Vichhea Chea,Luc Vinet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维正方形格子的三角形区域上构造了Smorodinsky–Winternitz I超可积系统的有限离散实现，基于一对具有有限谱的交换数算子和阶梯算子结构，证明其最大超可积性及Hahn代数对称性，并利用Tratnik型二元对偶Hahn多项式精确求解谱问题，最终通过连续极限恢复连续模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将连续超可积系统离散化并保持其完整的可积结构（包括对称代数、阶梯算子、精确可解性）是一个重要的研究方向。Smorodinsky–Winternitz I系统作为重要的超可积系统，此前缺乏在有限离散格点上的忠实实现，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构造一对在三角形区域上具有有限谱的交换数算子，并引入关联的阶梯算子结构；利用该代数结构证明系统的最大超可积性，识别其对称代数为Hahn代数；通过Tratnik型二元对偶Hahn多项式对谱问题进行精确求解；最后通过连续极限分析验证与连续Smorodinsky–Winternitz I系统的一致性。

Result: 成功构造了有限离散的Smorodinsky–Winternitz I系统，证明其最大超可积性并建立了Hahn代数对称结构；谱问题由Tratnik型二元对偶Hahn多项式完全刻画；连续极限精确恢复连续模型及其阶梯算子、本征函数和对称代数。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了该离散模型是连续Smorodinsky–Winternitz I系统的真正有限离散实现，为离散超可积系统的构造与理解提供了新范例。

Abstract: We construct a finite discrete realization of the Smorodinsky--Winternitz I superintegrable system on a triangular region of the two-dimensional square lattice. The construction is based on a pair of commuting number operators with finite spectrum together with an associated ladder-operator structure. We show that the resulting model is maximally superintegrable and that its symmetry algebra admits a Hahn-algebra presentation. Its spectral problem is solved exactly in terms of the bivariate dual Hahn polynomials of Tratnik type. Finally, we show that the continuum limit recovers the continuous Smorodinsky--Winternitz I system together with its ladder operators, eigenfunctions and symmetry algebra, thereby establishing the present construction as a genuine finite discrete realization of the continuous model.

</details>


### [118] [Notes on Electrostatics in $\mathbb R^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12900)
*Vladimir Gol'dshtein,Wolfgang H. Müller,Reuven Segev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种电静力学的紧凑表述，在不使用本构关系（包括以太关系）的前提下，通过电荷势场描述电荷分布，并基于虚功原理推导出机械力泛函，该泛函包含不对称主动应力场的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电静力学公式依赖于本构关系和以太等假设，本文旨在提出一种更一般化的表述，不依赖这些约束条件，从而更普遍地描述带电区域在势函数下的力学行为。

Method: 提出电荷势场来描述电荷分布，给定带电区域在势函数下的势能表达式（不借助本构关系）；假设电荷虚运动过程中场做功等于势能对时间的负导数（虚功原理），由此推导出机械力泛函。

Result: 推导出的机械力泛函包含不对称主动应力场D_i E_j的作用，其反对称部分是机械力偶密度。

Conclusion: 该紧凑表述展示了对电静力学系统中力学力的新理解，特别是揭示了不对称主动应力及其力偶密度的作用，为不依赖传统本构关系的电动力学理论提供了一条新途径。

Abstract: A compact formulation of electrostatics is presented. Without using constitutive relations, including the aether relations, an expression for the potential energy of a charged region under a potential function is proposed, where the charge distribution is specified by the charge potential field. Assuming that during a virtual motion of the charge, the virtual work expended by the field is equal to minus the time derivative of the potential energy, an expression for the mechanical force functional is derived. The force functional contains the action of an asymmetric active stress field D_{i}E_{j}, the skew-symmetric part of which is the mechanical couple density.

</details>


### [119] [A discrete Smorodinsky--Winternitz II superintegrable system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12909)
*Pierre-Antoine Bernard,Vutha Vichhea Chea,Luc Vinet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维正方形格子的三角形区域上构造了一个离散的Smorodinsky–Winternitz II超可积系统，该系统由一对具有有限谱的交换有限差分数算符及相伴的阶梯算符结构构建，证明其最大超可积性且对称代数具有Hahn代数表示，谱问题通过混合Krawtchouk与双Hahn型的双变量正交多项式精确求解，并展示了连续极限恢复连续SW-II系统及其Hermite–Laguerre本征函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将连续超可积系统离散化并保持其代数结构，是离散可积系统研究的重要课题。Smorodinsky–Winternitz II系统是经典的超可积系统，构造其离散版本并保持最大超可积性与精确可解性，有助于理解离散与连续极限下的对称代数结构对应关系。

Method: 从一对交换的有限差分number operators出发，构建阶梯算符结构，在三角形网格区域上定义离散SW-II系统；通过对称代数分析给出Hahn代数表示；利用因子化的A2-Leonard对相关联的混合Krawtchouk–dual Hahn型双变量正交多项式求解谱问题；通过连续极限分析恢复连续系统并研究代数结构的变化。

Result: 证明了该离散系统是最大超可积的，其对称代数具有Hahn代数表示；谱问题通过混合Krawtchouk和dual Hahn型的双变量正交多项式精确求解；连续极限恢复了连续SW-II系统及其Hermite–Laguerre本征函数，同时发现Hahn表示在此极限下变得奇异，而极限代数结构由与Cartesian和抛物分离变量相关的Laguerre–Heun代数自然地描述。

Conclusion: 成功构造了一个离散SW-II超可积系统并完整解决其谱问题，揭示了离散与连续极限之间对称代数的微妙转换关系——从Hahn代数过渡到Laguerre–Heun代数，为离散超可积系统及其连续极限的代数结构研究提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We construct a discrete Smorodinsky--Winternitz II superintegrable system on a triangular region of the two-dimensional square lattice. The model is built from a pair of commuting finite-difference number operators with finite spectrum together with an associated ladder-operator structure. We show that it is maximally superintegrable and that its symmetry algebra admits a Hahn-algebra presentation. The spectral problem is solved exactly in terms of bivariate orthogonal polynomials of mixed Krawtchouk and dual Hahn type associated with the factorized $A_2$-Leonard pair. Finally, we show that the continuum limit recovers the continuous Smorodinsky--Winternitz II system together with its Hermite--Laguerre eigenfunctions. We further explain how the Hahn presentation of the discrete symmetry algebra becomes singular in this limit, while the limiting algebraic structure is naturally described by the Laguerre--Heun algebra associated with Cartesian and parabolic separation of variables.

</details>


### [120] [Transparent Boundary Conditions for the Heat Equation on Metric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12937)
*Jasur Matrasulov,Jambul Yusupov,Matthias Ehrhardt*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了度量星形图上时变热方程的透明边界条件（TBCs），通过将TBC概念与PDE网络理论结合，推导出确保热量在连接点无阻碍流动的精确顶点条件，并提出了消除顶点热回流的扩散系数求和规则，最后用Crank-Nicolson有限差分法数值验证了该模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分支准一维结构（如度量星形图）中研究热扩散时，连接点（顶点）处的边界条件会干扰热流的自然传播。现有的TBC方法主要针对规则域，缺乏适用于复杂网络拓扑的理论框架。作者希望建立一个数学模型，既能保证热流在网络中无阻碍传播，又能为低维结构中的热扩散提供可调的优化控制工具。

Method: 将经典透明边界条件（TBCs）概念与度量图上的PDE理论相结合，在星形图各分支的顶点处推导精确的顶点条件。通过数学分析得出扩散系数满足特定求和规则的充分条件，以消除顶点处的热回流；随后采用Crank-Nicolson有限差分法进行数值离散，验证解析条件在实际计算中能够实现平滑无阻碍的热传播。

Result: 推导出了精确的顶点条件及扩散系数的求和规则，数值结果表明该模型能够确保热流在星形网络连接点处平滑、无阻碍地传播，验证了理论条件的正确性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法为低维网络结构中的热扩散提供了实用的数学框架，可作为可调的热扩散控制工具，有望应用于热管理和热能优化等工程领域。

Abstract: We study transparent boundary conditions (TBCs) for the time-dependent heat equation in a branching, quasi-one-dimensional domain modeled as a metric star graph. By combining the classical concept of TBCs with the theory of partial differential equations (PDEs) on networks, we derive an exact vertex condition that ensures unobstructed thermal flow across junctions. Specifically,we derive a sum rule for the diffusion coefficients that eliminates thermal backflow at the vertex.We demonstrate the validity of this analytical model numerically using a Crank-Nicolson finite-difference method, which confirms smooth, unobstructed heat propagation through the network.These results provide a practical mathematical framework for tunable control and optimization of thermal diffusion in low-dimensional structures. In particular, combining the well-known concept of the TBCs and theory for PDE on metric graphs, we propose a mathematical model providing a control tool for thermal diffusion in networks.

</details>


### [121] [Hidden Dyson Universality in Inverse-Spectral Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13475)
*Momo Hayashi,Kazumitsu Sakai*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现Dyson普适性在非线性逆谱重建后仍保留于重建算子的矩阵几何中。通过dressing变换将每个展开谱映射为谐振子的形变f(x)，并用距离壳层矩(d=|m-n|)刻画矩阵元权重分布。该诊断以Gaussian β系综标定，能区分GOE/GUE/GSE，并判定Riemann zeta函数非平凡零点属于GUE扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为Dyson普适性仅体现在局部本征值统计中。作者试图探究在非线性逆谱重建过程中，这一普适性特征是否仍然存活，并能否通过重建算子的几何结构来重新识别谱统计的对称性类别。

Method: 1) 利用dressing变换将每个展开谱映射为固定谐振子的形变函数f(x)；2) 在共同谐振子基底下用矩阵元F_{mn}=<m|f|n>表示该形变；3) 将矩阵元按固定距离d=|m-n|分解为能量传递通道壳层；4) 用距离壳层矩(distribution moments)刻画壳层分布；5) 在Gaussian β系综中标定该诊断工具，验证其对β的平滑变化及对GOE/GUE/GSE的区分能力。

Result: 距离壳层矩随β平滑变化并能清晰区分GOE、GUE和GSE三个对称类。将该诊断应用于Riemann zeta函数非平凡零点时，重建算子被判定属于GUE扇区，从而在不直接使用输入能级统计特征的情况下，仅通过重建算子的距离分辨几何恢复了零点的GUE特征。

Conclusion: Dyson普适性不仅可以体现在局部本征值统计中，还能通过逆谱重建传播并显现在重建算子的矩阵几何上。距离壳层矩是一种有效的不变量型诊断工具，可用于谱对称性分类，且Riemann zeta零点明确落入GUE类别，为理解其普适性本质提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: Dyson universality typically manifests itself in local eigenvalue statistics. Here we show that its signature survives a nonlinear inverse-spectral reconstruction and reappears in the matrix geometry of the reconstructed operator. Using a dressing transformation, we map each unfolded spectrum to a deformation $f(x)$ of a fixed harmonic oscillator and represent it in the common oscillator basis by $F_{mn}=\bra m|f|n\ket$. We resolve the matrix-element weight into shells of fixed distance $d=|m-n|$, corresponding to the energy-transfer channels of the reference oscillator, and characterize the resulting distribution by distance-shell moments. Independently calibrated on Gaussian $β$-ensembles, these moments vary smoothly with $β$ and distinguish the GOE, GUE, and GSE. With this calibration fixed, applying the same diagnostic to the nontrivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function places the reconstructed operators in the GUE sector. Thus, the GUE character of the zeros is recovered not through direct statistics of the input levels, but from the distance-resolved geometry of the reconstructed operator.

</details>


### [122] [Exact analytical solution for the non-selfadjoint problem of Maxwell--Cattaneo--Vernotte heat conduction with heat-transfer boundary condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13164)
*Mátyás Szücs,Tamás Fülöp*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文解析求解了具有传热边界条件的Maxwell-Cattaneo-Vernotte（MCV）方程，通过建立伴随特征值问题构造双正交系统，获得了任意平方可积初始条件下的无穷级数解析解，并系统研究了谱结构、参数影响及与数值解的对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越傅里叶热传导模型（特别是MCV方程）在传热边界条件下的解析解尚属空白，因为该边界条件使微分算子不再自伴，传统方法失效。需要建立严格的双正交特征函数框架来获得解析解，以作为数值基准并揭示从傅里叶扩散到热波传播的过渡机制。

Method: 针对传热边界条件下非自伴的MCV方程算子，引入合适的标量积构造伴随特征值问题；利用左右特征函数构成的双正交系统确定任意平方可积初始条件的展开系数；对平衡和闪光脉冲两种初始条件进行解析推导，并对真实、虚数及复共轭特征值根的谱结构进行严格分析；通过有限差分数值解进行验证。

Result: 获得了便于使用的无穷和解析解，深入研究了接近傅里叶极限与高双曲性区域的各种参数行为；详细分析了无量纲松弛时间与Biot数对特征值谱（实根、虚根、复共轭根及其渐近分布）的影响；与有限差分数值解吻合良好，同时阐明了谱截断引起的Gibbs现象和热波阵面附近的数值耗散效应；数值验证了特征函数的完备性。

Conclusion: 该工作为非自伴双曲热传导提供了解析基准，揭示了边界传热与松弛时间如何协同控制傅里叶型扩散与热波传播之间的转变，为在实验中研究超越傅里叶热传导开辟了途径。

Abstract: The most well-known beyond-Fourier heat conduction model, the Maxwell--Cattaneo--Vernotte equation is solved analytically in the presence of heat transfer boundary condition. In contrast to the corresponding Fourier problem, this boundary condition renders the underlying differential operator non-selfadjoint. With a suitable scalar product, the adjoint eigenvalue problem is established. The resulting left and right eigenfunctions constitute a biorthogonal system, allowing the expansion coefficients to be determined from arbitrary square-integrable initial conditions. This enables a convenient infinite-sum analytical solution, which is presented and thoroughly investigated for various values of the model parameters (including near-Fourier and highly hyperbolic regimes) and for two practically important initial conditions (equilibrium and flash pulse initiated). The spectral structure is analyzed in detail, including the occurrence of real, imaginary, and complex-conjugate eigenvalue roots, their asymptotic distribution, and their dependence on the dimensionless relaxation time and Biot number. We find good agreement with corresponding finite-difference numerical solutions. The comparison also illustrates the effects of spectral truncation (i.e., the Gibbs phenomenon) and numerical dissipation near propagating thermal-wave fronts. Completeness of the eigenfunction set is numerically demonstrated. The initial condition induced by the flash pulse is derived analytically. The results provide an analytical benchmark for non-selfadjoint hyperbolic heat conduction, unveil how boundary heat transfer and relaxation time influence the transition between Fourier-like diffusion and thermal-wave propagation, and open the possibility to find and investigate beyond-Fourier heat conduction via heat transfer in experiments and practical applications.

</details>


### [123] [Symmetry Emergence in Self-Organized Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13500)
*Ernesto Lupercio,Mikhail Shkolnikov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了自组织临界性原型模型中仿射对称性涌现的机制：当晶格网格倒数的平方远大于按给定概率测度分布的随机扰动点数时，倾倒函数（odometer）的标度极限是Monge-Ampère方程的唯一凹解，从而可以精确估计任意宏观窗口内密度相对于最大值的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自组织临界性（SOC）系统中在最大密度区域如何涌现某种对称性（仿射对称性），以及如何精确刻画系统偏离临界密度时的行为。

Method: 采用归纳论证（已被提升为严格证明）及实证观察，证明倾倒函数标度极限为该凸域上以给定概率测度为势、带Dirichlet边界条件的Monge-Ampère方程的唯一凹解，该方程在最优输运和微分几何领域众所周知。

Result: 建立了仿射对称性涌现的机制，并得到了倾倒函数标度极限的精确刻画——即Monge-Ampère方程的唯一凹解，使得能够在任意宏观窗口内准确估计密度偏离最大值的偏差。

Conclusion: 仿射对称性涌现的根本原因在于：在最大密度区制下，倾倒函数的标度极限满足以给定概率测度为势的Monge-Ampère方程（带Dirichlet边界条件），这为精确估算密度偏差提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We describe a mechanism of affine symmetry emergence in the maximal density regime of the prototypical model of self-organized criticality when the inverse square of the mesh of the underlying lattice is much larger than the number of random perturbation points distributed according to a prescribed probability measure supported in the interior of the ambient convex domain. Moreover, an appropriate scaling limit of the toppling function (aka odometer), which counts the number of operations per site, is a solution to a non-linear partial differential equation well known in the context of optimal transport and differential geometry, making it possible to accurately estimate the deviation of the density from its maximal value in any macroscopic window. The mechanism for the affine symmetry emergence is due to the novel empirical fact, supported in addition by inductive arguments that have recently being upgraded to a rigorous proof, that the scaling limit of the toppling function is the unique concave solution of the Monge-Ampère equation with Dirichlet boundary condition on the convex domain with the potential given by the probability measure used above as the infinite-perturbation profile.

</details>


### [124] [On Toeplitz determinants with slow Fourier decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13182)
*Nedialko Bradinoff,Maurice Duits*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Fourier系数满足$f_k=O(|k|^{-1})$的符号的Toeplitz行列式$\det T_n(e^f)$，该框架超越了$H^{1/2}$空间并涵盖Fisher-Hartwig奇点。作者基于Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff公式发展了算子论方法，将二次项与高阶项分离，证明二次项决定行列式的增长，而高阶系数保持有界，并获得了CUE线性统计的中心极限定理及混合指数矩界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Toeplitz行列式渐近理论（如Widom定理）通常要求符号属于$H^{1/2}$空间，但实际应用中（如Fisher-Hartwig奇点）符号的Fourier系数衰减更慢，仅满足$f_k=O(|k|^{-1})$。现有方法不足以处理这一更广的符号类，需要发展新的技术来刻画行列式的增长行为与高阶项的渐近性质。

Method: 采用基于Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff (BCH)公式的算子论方法。通过BCH公式将对数行列式$\log\det T_n(e^{tf})$展开为幂级数，其中二次项$\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\min(k,n)f_kf_{-k}$被分离出来，其余为高阶项。该方法使得可以分别处理二次项的贡献（随n增长）和高阶项的贡献（有界）。

Result: （1）证明了二次项完全刻画行列式随n的可能增长，而每个固定的高阶系数保持有界；（2）对正负Fourier部分有界的符号，在去除二次贡献后给出了行列式的双边界；（3）对包括Fisher-Hartwig型在内的更广可容许符号类，获得了高阶系数的均匀界以及CUE线性统计的中心极限定理；（4）获得了超越特征多项式的CUE衍生随机场的混合指数矩界。

Conclusion: 该工作将Toeplitz行列式的精细渐近分析推广到超越$H^{1/2}$的符号类，为处理Fisher-Hartwig奇点提供了新的统一框架，并建立了与随机矩阵理论（CUE线性统计）的深刻联系。BCH公式方法有望成为处理更一般Toeplitz行列式问题的有效工具。

Abstract: We study Toeplitz determinants $\det T_n(e^f)$ for $f$ whose Fourier coefficients satisfy $f_k=O(|k|^{-1})$. This regime extends beyond $H^{1/2}$ and includes symbols with Fisher-Hartwig singularities. We develop an operator-theoretic approach based on the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff formula that separates the quadratic term \[ \sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\min(k,n)f_kf_{-k} \] from the higher-order terms in the expansion of $\log\det T_n(e^{tf})$. We show that this quadratic term accounts for the possible growth with $n$, while every fixed higher-order coefficient remains bounded. For symbols with bounded positive and negative Fourier parts, our estimates yield two-sided bounds for the determinant after removal of the quadratic contribution. For a broader admissible class, including Fisher-Hartwig-type symbols, we obtain uniform higher-order coefficient bounds and a central limit theorem for the associated CUE linear statistics. We also obtain bounds on mixed exponential moments for CUE-derived random fields beyond the characteristic polynomial.

</details>


### [125] [Proper condensates and the Onsager-Penrose condition: A friendly debate on Bose-Einstein condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13402)
*Detlev Buchholz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文以辩论形式对比了proper凝聚与Onsager-Penrose判据两种玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚的概念，并通过序言和结语介绍了相关背景及resolvent代数的物理性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚存在多种定义方式，其中proper凝聚与Onsager-Penrose判据各有其理论立场和适用场景。本文旨在通过辩论的形式厘清两种概念之间的区别与联系，并揭示其在数学物理框架下的深层含义。

Method: 采用辩论形式，由两位分别支持proper凝聚和Onsager-Penrose判据的学者展开论述。序言简要介绍两种判据，结语则讨论与之相关的resolvent代数的性质及其物理相关性。

Result: 文章通过辩论揭示了两种凝聚判据的差异，并在结语中指出了resolvent代数的某些性质对理解此问题具有物理意义。

Conclusion: proper凝聚与Onsager-Penrose判据代表了玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚的两种不同理解方式，而resolvent代数提供了进一步分析这些概念的数学工具和物理洞见。

Abstract: This article contrasts the concepts of a proper condensate and the Onsager-Penrose criterion for Bose-Einstein condensation in the form of a debate between two proponents of the respective concepts. The prologue briefly introduces the two criteria. The epilogue contains remarks on properties of the underlying resolvent algebra that are physically relevant in this context.

</details>

### [126] [Distance Matrices of Ordered Point Clouds and Their Persistent Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12620)
*David Hien*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文建立了时间序列的距离矩阵过滤与其状态空间嵌入的Čech（或Vietoris–Rips）过滤之间的一阶链映射关系，研究由此诱导的同调映射，并证明该映射可用于简化图像持久性计算，以获得更精细的骑车运动类型转换分析信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 距离矩阵可用热图可视化，时间序列的次水平集即复现图（recurrence plots），广泛应用于时间序列分析。作者希望从这一视角出发，建立距离矩阵过滤与状态空间嵌入过滤之间的理论联系，并利用该联系简化图像持久性（image persistence）计算，从而为时间序列分析（特别是cycling signatures）提供更高效且更精细的工具。

Method: 作者将时间序列的距离矩阵视为图像，其次水平集形成过滤；同时考虑时间序列状态空间嵌入的Čech（或Vietoris–Rips）过滤。通过在两者之间构造一个度为一的链映射（degree-one chain map），研究其在同调层面诱导的映射，证明从H_0到H_1的映射本质上是满射，并给出一个从H_1到H_2映射非平凡的实例。

Result: 建立了距离矩阵过滤与状态空间嵌入过滤之间的链映射及其同调映射性质：H_0到H_1的映射本质上是满射，且存在H_1到H_2映射非平凡的示例。这些链映射可应用于简化cycling signatures计算中的图像持久性计算，并获得更精细的信息以分析不同骑车运动类型之间的转换。

Conclusion: 该工作从拓扑视角统一了距离矩阵（复现图）过滤与状态空间嵌入过滤，提供了计算上的简化途径，并拓宽了拓扑工具在时间序列分析中的应用，特别是在刻画骑车运动模式转换方面的能力。

Abstract: The distance matrix of a finite point cloud can be visualized as a heatmap. When the data arise from a time series, the sublevel sets of this image are known as recurrence plots and are widely used in time series analysis. Motivated by this perspective, we establish a relationship between the distance-matrix filtration of the time series and the Čech (or Vietoris--Rips) filtration of its state-space embedding in the form of a degree-one chain map. We study the induced maps in homology, showing that the map from $H_0$ into $H_1$ is essentially surjective and providing an example where the map from $H_1$ into $H_2$ is nontrivial. These chain maps can be applied to simplify image persistence computations arising in the computation of cycling signatures, a topological tool for time series analysis. Moreover, these computations yield finer information that allows the analysis of transitions between different types of cycling motion.

</details>
