### [127] [Dark matter in scale-invariant gravity with hidden-sector condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12456)
*Juan P. Garcés,Jisuke Kubo,Manfred Lindner,Markus Reinig*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的框架，将电弱能标的起源、宇宙暴胀和暗物质三个看似独立的问题联系起来，基于经典标度不变的二次引力（R²项）与强耦合隐藏扇区的耦合。R²项自然实现Starobinsky暴胀，隐藏扇区的禁闭动态生成普朗克尺度并通过引力诱导的Higgs质量生成触发电弱对称破缺，随后通过引力冻结产生机制再加热可见和隐藏扇区。文中考察了三种隐藏扇区实现（隐藏η'介子、隐藏矢量玻色子、带电隐藏π介子），并推导了相应的暗物质和暗辐射丰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型中电弱能标起源（层级问题）、宇宙暴胀机制和暗物质本质通常被视为三个独立的问题，分别需要不同的新物理方案。作者希望探索一个统一框架，用一个共同的底层理论同时解释这三个宇宙学和粒子物理的核心谜题。

Method: 采用经典标度不变的二次引力理论（含R²项），与一个强相互作用的隐藏扇区耦合。R²标量自由度实现Starobinsky暴胀；隐藏扇区的禁闭/手征对称性破缺动态生成普朗克尺度，并引力诱导Higgs质量负项触发电弱对称破缺；R²标量通过普适的能量-动量张量耦合衰变再加热两个扇区，通过引力冻结产生机制产生隐藏扇区粒子。分析了三种代表性隐藏扇区模型，并计算暗物质与暗辐射的遗迹丰度。

Result: 在三种隐藏扇区实现中均获得了可行的暗物质候选体和相应的暗物质/暗辐射遗迹丰度，说明该统一框架能够同时自洽地解释电弱能标、暴胀和暗物质现象。

Conclusion: 电弱能标、宇宙暴胀和暗物质可以统一地源于经典标度不变的二次引力与强耦合隐藏扇区的框架，这一方案为超越标准模型的理论构建提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: The origin of the electroweak scale, cosmic inflation, and dark matter are often treated as independent problems beyond the Standard Models of particle physics and cosmology. In this work, we explore the possibility that they instead arise from a common underlying framework based on classically scale-invariant quadratic gravity coupled to a strongly interacting hidden sector. The $R^2$ term naturally realizes Starobinsky inflation, while confinement in the hidden sector dynamically generates the Planck scale and triggers electroweak symmetry breaking through a gravitationally induced Higgs mass generation mechanism. The scalar degree of freedom associated with the $R^2$ term subsequently reheats both the visible and hidden sectors through universal couplings to the energy-momentum tensor, leading to the gravitational freeze-in production of hidden-sector states. We investigate three representative realizations of the hidden sector in which the dark matter candidate is either a hidden $η'$ meson, a hidden vector boson, or charged hidden pions, and derive the corresponding dark matter and dark radiation relic abundances.

</details>


### [128] [Probing CP-violating top-quark dipole moments with tomographic observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12463)
*Maxim Perelstein,Minal Shaik,Taewook Youn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子层析技术，通过双轻子角关联重建顶夸克对的全自旋密度矩阵，用于探测顶夸克耦合中的CP破坏，并构造了最优可观测量，在LHC和FCC-ee上对电偶极矩算符的灵敏度显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶夸克对产生过程是探测新物理特别是CP破坏的黄金场所，但传统可观测量对CP破坏算符的灵敏度有限。作者旨在利用量子层析方法重构完整的自旋密度矩阵，从对称性角度识别对CP破坏敏感的可观测量，从而超越传统方法的灵敏度极限。

Method: 该方法包括：(1) 从e+e-和强子对撞机上的双轻子角关联中重建顶夸克对的全自旋密度矩阵；(2) 利用对称性论证识别对CP破坏敏感的层析可观测量；(3) 研究这些可观测量对(色)电偶极矩算符的灵敏度；(4) 构造结合层析与运动学信息的最优可观测量，对每个算符和产生过程实现统计最优灵敏度；(5) 基于快速探测器模拟分析顶夸克对产生与双轻子道衰变。

Result: 在95%置信水平下，对电弱电偶极矩算符的新物理尺度灵敏度在FCC-ee（365 GeV, 3 ab^-1）上为10-15 TeV；对顶夸克色电偶极矩在LHC Run 2和HL-LHC上分别达到40 TeV和200 TeV。这些灵敏度均超过实验目前使用的传统可观测量。

Conclusion: 量子层析方法在搜索CP破坏方面具有显著优势，其灵敏度超越传统可观测量，展示了该方法的强大潜力，为顶夸克耦合的CP破坏探测提供了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum tomography program reconstructs the full spin density matrix of top-quark pairs from dilepton angular correlations at both electron-positron and hadron colliders. We use symmetry arguments to identify tomographic observables sensitive to CP violation in top quark couplings. As a concrete example, we study the sensitivity of these observables to (chromo-)electric dipole moment operators at the LHC and the FCC-ee. We also construct the optimal observable, which combines tomographic and kinematic information to achieve statistically optimal sensitivity for each operator and production process. The analysis is based on fast detector simulation of top pair production and decay in the dilepton channel. We project a $95\%$ CL sensitivity to new-physics scales (the inverse dipole couplings) of $10-15$ TeV for the electroweak electric dipoles at the FCC-ee ($365$ GeV, $3~{\rm ab}^{-1}$) and of 40 (200) TeV for the top chromo-electric dipole moment at the LHC Run 2 (HL-LHC). This sensitivities exceed those of the traditional observables currently used by experiments to use for these operators, indicating the power of the tomographic approach to search for CP violation.

</details>


### [129] [Soft Collinear Effective Theory for Heavy QCD Axions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12467)
*Deepanshu Bisht,Sabyasachi Chakraborty,Siddhartha Karmakar,Atanu Samanta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个用于重QCD轴子的软共线有效理论（SCET）框架，以B→Ka为基准过程研究了两种低能实现。在第一种实现中（aGĞ是唯一独立轴子相互作用），消除冗余的味改变导数-胶子算符会生成新的七维轴子算符O∂ag，其对谱散射贡献约为软贡献的25%。在第二种实现中（Wilson系数在电弱尺度以上存在），RG演化和匹配诱导出直接的b→sa算符，产生主导的软形状因子贡献，而胶子谱项仅占约6-7%。文章最终给出了两种实现下轴子衰变常数fa的约束并比较了它们的唯象学意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重轴子（heavy QCD axion）在稀有B介子衰变（如B→Ka）中的产生机制尚未在SCET框架下被系统研究，特别是谱散射（spectator scattering）贡献在重轴子产生中的作用与条件尚不明确，需要建立严格的有效场论框架来区分不同低能实现下的主导贡献并推导相应的物理约束。

Method: 作者构建了针对重QCD轴子的SCET框架，在μ~m_b能标下考虑两种低能实现：(1) 仅保留aGĞ作为独立轴子相互作用时，通过在弱有效理论中消除冗余的味改变导数-胶子算符，推导出新的七维轴子算符O∂ag，并将其匹配到SCET，计算leading-power的软贡献和谱散射贡献，后者可用微扰硬核和B、K介子光锥分布振幅因子化表达；(2) 假设aGĞ的Wilson系数在电弱尺度以上存在，通过RG演化和匹配得到直接的b→sa算符，进而得到软形状因子主导的贡献。

Result: 在第一种实现中，谱散射贡献与软重叠项在同一幂次阶出现，约为软贡献的25%；在第二种实现中，直接b→sa算符导致软形状因子贡献占主导，胶子谱项仅为数值次主导（约6-7%）。文章据此识别了谱散射在稀有B衰变中产生重轴子变得重要的条件，并给出两种实现下轴子衰变常数fa的下限约束。

Conclusion: 通过系统的SCET分析，本文明确区分了两种低能实现下重轴子产生的物理图像：在第一种实现中谱散射贡献不可忽略（~25%），而在第二种实现中其贡献次主导（~6-7%），从而阐明了谱散射贡献在何种条件下对重轴子产生具有重要意义，并给出了相应的fa约束和唯象学比较。

Abstract: We develop a soft-collinear effective theory (SCET) framework for heavy QCD axion, considering two low-energy realizations and taking $B\to Ka$ as a benchmark mode. In the first realization, $aG\widetilde G$ is assumed to be the only independent axion interaction at the scale $μ\sim m_b$. We show that eliminating the redundant flavor-changing derivative-gluon operator in the weak effective theory generates a new dimension-seven axion operator identified as $\mathrm{O}_{\partial ag}$. We match this operator onto SCET and derive the corresponding leading-power soft and spectator-scattering contributions to $B\to Ka$. We obtain a factorized expression for the spectator contribution in terms of perturbative hard kernels and the $B$- and $K$-meson light-cone distribution amplitudes. The spectator contribution arises at the same order in the power expansion as the soft-overlap term and amounts to approximately $25\%$ of the soft contribution. In the second realization, the Wilson coefficient of $aG\widetilde G$ is assumed to be present above the electroweak scale. Renormalization-group evolution and matching then induce a direct $b\to sa$ operator, which subsequently results in a dominant soft form-factor contribution, whereas the gluonic spectator term turns out to be numerically subleading ($\sim 6-7\%$). We thus identify the conditions under which spectator scattering becomes relevant for heavy-axion production in rare $B$-meson decays. Finally, we derive the corresponding bounds on the axion decay constant $f_a$ for both realizations and compare their phenomenological implications.

</details>


### [130] [Defect-Mediated Conversion: Dark Matter from Cosmological Domain-Wall Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12468)
*João Paulo Pinheiro,Mohamed Younes Sassi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的暗物质产生机制——缺陷介导转化（DMC）。宇宙学畴壁核心中的标量凝聚体可作为标准模型与暗扇区之间的混合门户，热浴费米子穿过畴壁时会被部分非热转化成暗粒子。转化概率由单一无量纲混合面积决定，产额由网络面积密度决定。轻暗粒子的转化概率与入射能量和暗质量无关，遗迹丰度坍缩为一条从keV Lyman-α下限到壁形成尺度的单一质量-耦合关系；重暗粒子则通过转化将单个入射费米子的能量转化为暗质量，使暗扇区质量可超过浴温度和媒介子质量。该门户同样驱动普通freeze-in，但DMC凭借相干壁面积增强在固定耦合下高出1-2个数量级，并在freeze-in失效的重质量区仍保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暗物质产生机制（如freeze-in）在高暗质量区会失效，且缺乏新的非热产生途径。作者希望提供一种新的暗物质生成机制，既能覆盖从keV到远超浴温度的宽质量区间，又能在固定耦合下超越freeze-in效率，从而拓展暗物质模型的参数空间。

Method: 提出畴壁核心标量凝聚体作为混合门户的机制：热浴费米子穿过畴壁时部分转化为暗粒子。转化概率由壁轮廓决定的无量纲混合面积控制，产额由畴壁网络的面积密度决定。作者将轻粒子区（与能量和暗质量无关）与重粒子区（单粒子能量转化为暗质量）统一处理，并与标准freeze-in门户对比。

Result: 轻暗粒子情形下遗迹丰度坍缩为单一质量-耦合关系，覆盖keV Lyman-α下限至壁形成尺度；重暗粒子情形可产生超过浴温度和媒介子质量的暗扇区粒子。DMC在固定耦合下比freeze-in高1-2个数量级，且在freeze-in关闭的重质量区域仍保持有效。

Conclusion: Defect-Mediated Conversion为暗物质产生提供了一种新的非热机制，拓展了暗物质质量-耦合参数空间，尤其在高质量区域弥补了freeze-in的失效，是一种有竞争力的暗物质产生途径。

Abstract: We propose a new mechanism for dark matter genesis, \emph{Defect-Mediated Conversion} (DMC). Cosmological domain walls can host a scalar condensate in their core acting as a mixing portal between the Standard Model and a dark sector: thermal-bath fermions crossing the wall are partially and non-thermally converted into dark particles. The conversion probability is governed by a single dimensionless mixing area built from the wall profile, and the yield by the area density of the network. For a light dark particle the probability is independent of both the incident energy and the dark mass, and the relic abundance collapses onto a single mass--coupling relation running from the keV Lyman-$α$ floor to the wall-formation scale. For a heavy one, conversion turns the energy of a single incident fermion into the dark mass, populating dark sectors with masses exceeding both the bath temperature and the mediator mass. The same portal also sources ordinary freeze-in, but DMC, boosted by the coherent wall-area enhancement, dominates it by one to two orders of magnitude at fixed couplings and remains operative in the heavy regime, where freeze-in shuts off.

</details>


### [131] [Singlet-doublet dark matter beyond freeze-out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12480)
*Prudhvi N. Bhattiprolu,Aaron Pierce,Alexander Takla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文系统分析了Singlet-Doublet费米子暗物质模型在小Yukawa耦合极限下实现暗物质丰度的多种机制，重点关注冻入（freeze-in）和SuperWIMP等小耦合机制，并探讨了该参数区域下的对撞机物理现象学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Singlet-Doublet费米子暗物质模型是弱尺度暗物质的经济模型，其暗物质丰度可通过多种机制产生（冻出、共湮灭、共散射、冻入、SuperWIMP）。此前研究多集中于大Yukawa耦合的冻出区域，对小耦合区域（该区域直接和间接探测均困难，但对撞机现象学丰富）缺乏系统分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 论文系统梳理了Singlet-Doublet费米子模型中暗物质丰度的各种产生机制，依据Yukawa耦合强度对参数空间进行分类，重点聚焦小Yukawa耦合极限下的暗物质丰度实现路径，并对该区域的对撞机现象学信号进行分析。

Result: 展示了在小Yukawa耦合区域，冻出机制失效，而冻入和SuperWIMP机制可以成功产生观测到的暗物质密度，同时该参数区域虽难以通过直接和间接探测验证，但具备丰富的对撞机现象学信号可供研究。

Conclusion: Singlet-Doublet费米子暗物质模型在广泛参数空间内均可实现正确的暗物质丰度，小Yukawa耦合区域为代表的弱耦合场景是对撞机实验的重要探测目标，为暗物质实验研究提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: The Singlet-Doublet fermion model of dark matter is an economical weak-scale dark matter model that realizes the dark matter abundance through interactions with the electroweak bosons of the Standard Model. Depending on the size of the Yukawa couplings in the model, the dark matter relic abundance can be produced via freeze-out (including co-annihilation), co-scattering, freeze-in, or the SuperWIMP mechanism. We analyze the ways this model can realize the dark matter density with emphasis on the small Yukawa coupling regime. In this limit, direct and indirect detection are difficult, but a rich collider phenomenology is possible.

</details>


### [132] [Singlet-Doublet fermion origin of dark matter, neutrino mass and inverse first-order electroweak phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12483)
*Debasish Borah,Indrajit Saha,Sujit Kumar Sahoo,Narendra Sahu,Shashwat Sharma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类scotogenic型辐射中微子质量模型中逆一级电弱相变（IFOEWPT）与可观测引力波的可能性。模型在SM基础上扩展了两代SU(2)_L单态-双重态费米子、一个标量单态和三个右手中微子，轻中微子为Dirac型。较轻的SD费米子中性分量在残余Z_2对称性下稳定，作为暗物质候选者；较重的SD费米子与Higgs强耦合，驱动宇宙经历两次一级相变（IFOEWPT）。作者约束了参数空间以满足中微子质量、暗物质丰度和IFOEWPT要求，并纳入实验约束，指出最终允许的参数空间可在对撞机、直接探测、引力波和CMB实验中探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超越标准模型的新物理中，同时解释中微子质量起源、暗物质本质以及宇宙电弱相变（特别是逆一级相变）的可能性，并寻找可通过未来引力波实验观测的独特信号窗口。

Method: 在SG模型（scotogenic型）框架下扩展SM，引入两代单态-双重态费米子、一个单态标量及三个右手中微子；通过单圈图生成Dirac中微子质量；利用残差Z_2对称性稳定最轻SD费米子作为暗物质；分析较重SD费米子与Higgs强耦合驱动的IFOEWPT（宇宙经历两次一级相变），并综合中微子质量、暗物质丰度及实验限制对参数空间进行整体约束。

Result: 成功地在该模型框架下实现了IFOEWPT与辐射中微子质量的共存，获得了满足中微子质量、暗物质遗迹密度及IFOEWPT条件且兼容现有实验约束的允许参数空间，该参数空间具有对未来对撞机、直接探测、引力波和CMB实验的可探测性。

Conclusion: 该SD费米子扩展的scotogenic模型能够在统一框架内同时解释Dirac中微子质量、暗物质以及逆一级电弱相变，并且其预测的引力波信号和粒子物理现象有望在近期实验中被检验。

Abstract: We study the possibility of an inverse first-order electroweak phase transition (IFOEWPT) and observable gravitational waves (GW) in a radiative neutrino mass model of scotogenic type where singlet-doublet (SD) fermions, the lightest of whom is the dark matter (DM) candidate, generate the necessary seesaw at one-loop level. Considering the possibility of light neutrinos being Dirac for simplicity and additional detection prospects, we extend the standard model (SM) with two generations of $SU(2)_L$ singlet and doublet fermions, one singlet scalar, and three right-handed neutrinos (RHNs). While RHNs provide the right chiral parts of light Dirac neutrinos, the SD fermions and the scalar singlet facilitate the one-loop neutrino mass diagram. The neutral component of the lighter SD fermion, stabilized under a residual $Z_2$ symmetry plays the role of DM while the heavier SD fermions strongly couple to the Higgs leading to an IFOEWPT where the Universe undergoes two different first-order phase transition as it goes from the symmetric to the final broken Higgs phase. We constrain the parameter space from the requirements of generating the correct neutrino mass, DM relic as well as IFOEWPT while incorporating the existing constraints from different experiments. The final allowed parameter space of the model can be probed at collider, direct-detection, GW and cosmic microwave background (CMB) experiments in near future.

</details>


### [133] [Spontaneous Scoto-leptogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12497)
*Arghyajit Datta,Hyun Min Lee,Jun-Ho Song*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低标度自发轻子生成机制（Spontaneous Scoto-leptogenesis），在动力学最小Scotogenic模型中同时解释中微子质量、惰性标量暗物质和宇宙重子不对称性。通过全局U(1)_{B-L}对称性破缺产生的滚动Majoron，在B-L破坏相互作用存在下为右手型中微子提供有效的化学势，并通过电弱sphaleron转化产生观测到的宇宙重子不对称性。该机制在强洗刷（washout）区域有效，将最轻右手型中微子的质量尺度降低至TeV量级，并探索了惰性标量与Majoron构成的多组分暗物质图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的热学Scotogenic轻子生成机制需要较高标度的质量尺度，难以在现有实验中直接检验。作者希望构造一种低标度机制，使最轻右手型中微子降至TeV量级，从而同时解决中微子质量、暗物质和重子不对称性这三大未解之谜，并获得可被多种实验互补检验的可行性。

Method: 作者在最小Scotogenic模型中引入自发B-L对称性破缺，产生滚动Majoron场。由Majoron的时间演化诱导B-L电荷的有效化学势，配合B-L破坏相互作用使右手型中微子的衰变和逆衰变高效进行，从而在强洗刷区域实现自发轻子生成。基于λ5耦合精确控制惰性标量不对称性的擦除，并通过动力学错位（kinetic misalignment）机制探索Majoron作为暗物质组分的可能性。

Result: 该机制将最轻右手型中微子的可行质量尺度成功降低至TeV范围，使具有两个层级右手型中微子的热学Scotogenic轻子生成可直接被实验检验。λ5耦合在自发轻子生成和惰性标量暗物质中扮演关键角色。同时实现了含惰性标量和Majoron的多组分暗物质图像，为低标度重子生成、中微子质量和多组分暗物质提供了统一起源。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了低标度重子生成、中微子质量起源和多组分暗物质三个物理问题，可通过直接探测实验、惰性标量的对撞机搜寻以及未来对Majoron暗物质或暗辐射的探测进行互补性检验，为超越标准模型的新物理提供了可验证的途径。

Abstract: We propose a low-scale spontaneous leptogenesis scenario within the dynamical minimal scotogenic model for accommodating neutrino masses and inert scalar dark matter simultaneously. Thus, we dub the mechanism Spontaneous Scoto-leptogenesis. In this setup, a rolling Majoron arising from the global $U(1)_{B-L}$ symmetry breaking induces an effective chemical potential for the $B-L$ charge in the presence of $B-L$ violating interactions that allow for the efficient decays and inverse decays of right handed neutrinos (RHN), so it gives rise to the observed baryon asymmetry of the Universe through the electroweak sphaleron conversion. The mechanism becomes effective in the strong washout regime and successfully lowers the viable mass scale of the lightest RHN to the range of TeV scales, thereby making the thermal scotogenic leptogenesis with two hierarchical RHNs accessible to direct tests. We identify the roles of the $λ_5$ coupling for spontaneous leptogenesis and inert scalar dark matter through the efficient erasure of the inert scalar asymmetry. We also explore the regime for Majoron dark matter from the kinetic misalignment, showing that a multicomponent dark sector comprising the inert scalar and the Majoron can be realized in the model. The resulting framework provides a unified origin for low-scale baryogenesis, neutrino masses, and multicomponent dark sector, so it can be tested by complementary experimental probes through direct detection experiments, collider searches for inert scalars, and future detection of Majoron dark matter or dark radiation.

</details>


### [134] [N$^3$LO soft-gluon corrections for $Zγ$ production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12556)
*Nikolaos Kidonakis,Kaan Şimşek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了Zγ产生过程中的软胶子修正，达到N³LO阶（次次次领头阶），利用这些结果对LHC质子-质子碰撞中的Zγ产生截面给出近似N³LO（aN³LO）QCD预测。在13 TeV和13.6 TeV能量下计算了光子横动量p_T^γ和快度y^γ的微分截面，系统检查了高阶K因子，并量化了各微扰阶的尺度和PDF不确定性。研究发现软胶子修正在高p_T区域显著，aN³LO阶具有良好的微扰收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zγ产生过程是LHC的重要物理过程，涉及标准模型三规范玻色子耦合的直接检验，也是探测新物理的敏感探针。为了提高理论预测精度，需要更高阶的QCD修正，特别是高横动量区域软胶子效应显著，因此需要计算N³LO阶的软胶子修正以获得更精确的理论预言。

Method: 在一粒子包含（1PI）运动学下，计算Zγ产生过程的软胶子修正至N³LO阶。基于这些修正构造近似N³LO（aN³LO）QCD预测，计算LHC能级下质子-质子碰撞中Zγ产生的微分截面（光子横动量和快度分布），系统分析高阶K因子，并量化微扰各阶的尺度不确定性和PDF不确定性。

Result: 在13 TeV和13.6 TeV的LHC对撞能量下，获得了Zγ产生过程的光子p_T^γ和y^γ微分截面。软胶子修正在高p_T区域显著，对截面有重要贡献；aN³LO阶预测表现出良好的微扰收敛性；各阶的尺度不确定性和PDF不确定性均被量化。

Conclusion: 通过N³LO软胶子修正，显著提高了Zγ产生过程的理论预测精度，尤其在高p_T区域。aN³LO结果具有良好的微扰收敛性，为LHC实验测量Zγ过程提供了更可靠的理论基准。

Abstract: We calculate soft-gluon corrections for $Zγ$ production through next-to-next-to-next-to-leading-order (N$^3$LO) in one-particle-inclusive (1PI) kinematics. We use these results to produce approximate N$^3$LO (aN$^3$LO) QCD predictions for $Zγ$ production cross sections in proton-proton collisions at LHC energies. Differential cross sections in photon transverse momentum $p_T^γ$ and rapidity $y^γ$ are computed at 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV collider energies. Higher-order $K$-factors are examined systematically, and scale and PDF uncertainties are quantified at each perturbative order. Soft-gluon corrections are found to be significant in the high-$p_T$ region, with good perturbative convergence observed at aN$^3$LO.

</details>


### [135] [A Novel One-loop Model for Majorana Neutrino Mass and Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12646)
*Mohamed Belfkir,Mohamed Amin Loualidi,Salah Nasri*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了Majorana中微子质量有限单圈拓扑T4-3-i的完整场论实现。通过采用Dirac费米子中介子、将轻子数破坏置于圈内独立的Majorana费米子中，并施加精确Z₂守恒以保持新标量惰性，消除了树级I型或III型跷跷板对中微子质量的低阶贡献。作者对允许的弱荷分配进行了分类，并研究了最小单态-二重态实现(T4-3-i-B1)，其中包含一个Dirac费米子、一个Majorana费米子、一个惰性标量二重态和一个惰性标量单态。模型预测秩二的中微子质量矩阵，导致一个无质量中微子。作者全面约束了正常和倒置中微子质量次序，并考察了费米子与标量两种暗物质候选者的可行性，两者都依赖与惰性标量的共同湮灭来再现观测到的残余丰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T4-3-i是Weinberg算符的单圈实现拓扑，其中费米子连接两个外部的轻子-希格斯对。如果该费米子是Majorana单态或三重态，则会同时产生树级I型或III型跷跷板贡献，使圈图不再是中微子质量的领头阶来源，削弱了该拓扑的物理意义。作者旨在通过模型构建消除这种低阶贡献，从而获得T4-3-i拓扑的完整、自洽的场论实现，并系统检验该模型在多方面约束下的可行性。

Method: 作者将连接外部轻子-希格斯对的费米子选取为Dirac费米子N，将轻子数破坏引入圈内独立的Majorana费米子ψ中，并施加精确Z₂对称性使新标量保持惰性并稳定最轻奇宇称态。作者分类了所有允许的弱荷分配，确定了最小实现T4-3-i-B1（含一个Dirac费米子、一个Majorana费米子、一个惰性标量二重态和一个惰性标量单态），然后系统计算了中微子质量矩阵、味物理、电弱精度观测量、h→γγ、理论自洽性、残留丰度及直接探测截面等可观测量，并针对正常和倒置质量次序进行了数值分析。

Result: 该模型产生秩二的中微子质量矩阵，预言一个无质量中微子。在正常和倒置两种质量次序下，模型均能与中微子振荡与宇宙学数据、带电轻子味破坏（含μ-e转换）、电弱精度观测量、h→γγ以及理论自洽性约束相容。费米子和标量两种暗物质候选者均可行：费米子候选者仅有圈诱导的希格斯耦合，导致自旋无关散射率被强烈抑制；标量候选者通过树级希格斯门耦合，可位于中微子地板之上同时与当前直接探测限制兼容。两种情形下，与惰性标量的共同湮灭对再现观测残余丰度至关重要。

Conclusion: 作者首次完整实现了T4-3-i单圈拓扑，解决了该拓扑中树级跷跷板贡献压制圈图贡献的结构性困难。该框架不仅提供了一个自洽的中微子质量产生机制，还自然容纳了可行的费米子或标量暗物质候选者，且模型在广泛的实验与理论约束下仍然成立，为后续研究提供了具体的现象学蓝图。

Abstract: We present the first complete field-theoretic realization of the finite one-loop T4-3-i topology for Majorana neutrino mass. Here, T4-3-i denotes a one-loop realization of the Weinberg operator in which a fermion links the two external lepton--Higgs pairs. If this fermion is a Majorana singlet or triplet, the same interactions generate a tree-level type-I or type-III seesaw contribution, respectively, so that the loop is not the leading source of neutrino mass. This lower-order contribution is removed by taking the mediator to be a Dirac fermion $N$, placing lepton-number violation in a separate Majorana fermion $ψ$ inside the loop, and imposing an exact $Z_2$ symmetry that keeps the new scalars inert and stabilizes the lightest odd state. We classify the allowed electroweak charge assignments and study the minimal singlet-doublet realization, denoted T4-3-i-B1, which contains one Dirac fermion, one Majorana fermion, an inert scalar doublet, and an inert scalar singlet. The resulting rank-two neutrino mass matrix predicts one massless neutrino. We confront both normal and inverted neutrino-mass orderings with neutrino-oscillation and cosmological data, charged-lepton flavor violation, including $μ-e$ conversion, electroweak precision observables, $h\toγγ$, theoretical consistency conditions, the relic abundance, and direct-detection limits. Both fermionic and scalar dark matter are viable. The fermionic candidate has only a loop-induced Higgs coupling and consequently a strongly suppressed spin-independent scattering rate, whereas the scalar candidate couples through a tree-level Higgs portal and can lie above the neutrino floor while remaining compatible with current limits. In both cases, coannihilation with inert scalars is essential for reproducing the observed relic abundance.

</details>


### [136] [Massive cold hybrid stars in a modified Polyakov-Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12653)
*Sk Md Adil Imam,Pedro Costa,Mariana Dutra,Odilon Lourenço,Renan Pereira,Constança Providência*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种修正的Polyakov-loop Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (mPNJL)模型，其中Polyakov势显式依赖夸克化学势，使模型在零温下仍保持有限，从而描述冷致密物质中的禁闭-退禁闭相变。与强子物态方程通过Maxwell构造结合后，发现物态方程可呈现两种相变或单一相变路径，并能获得包含夸克物质核心的稳定大质量冷混合星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PNJL模型在低温高密度条件下Polyakov势趋于发散或失效，难以描述冷致密物质中的禁闭-退禁闭转变。作者旨在建立一个能在零温下有效工作的模型，以研究冷混合星中强子-夸克相变及其对星体结构的影响。

Method: 1) 构建mPNJL模型，赋予Polyakov势对夸克化学势的显式依赖以保持零温有限性；2) 通过Maxwell构造将修正的夸克扇区与强子物态方程相连接；3) 系统扫描模型参数（Polyakov势参数与夸克矢量相互作用强度），分析相变位置与混合星质量-半径关系；4) 计算最大质量构型中的声速以检验共形极限。

Result: 1) 根据参数不同，物态方程呈现两种相变（强子→禁闭/夸克物质→退禁闭夸克物质）或单一相变（强子→退禁闭夸克物质，或强子→夸克物质）；2) 排斥性矢量相互作用对获得稳定夸克核心至关重要；3) 在足够刚性的强子物态方程配合下，混合星可超过2倍太阳质量；4) 最大质量构型的夸克物质核心中声速可超过共形极限c_s²=1/3。

Conclusion: 该工作系统确立了各模型参数在塑造混合星结构中的定性作用，表明mPNJL模型能够自然产生禁闭（夸克物质）与退禁闭夸克核心两种混合星形态，且排斥矢量相互作用是支撑稳定大质量夸克核心的关键物理要素。

Abstract: We propose a modified Polyakov-loop Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (mPNJL) model in which the Polyakov potential is given by an explicit dependence on the quark chemical potential, allowing it to remain finite at zero temperature and thus to describe the confinement-deconfinement transition in cold dense matter. Combining this modified quark sector with hadronic equations of state via a Maxwell construction, we find that, depending on the model parameters, the equation of state can exhibit either two phase transitions, from hadronic matter to confined (quarkyonic) quark matter and subsequently to deconfined quark matter, or a single transition directly from hadronic to deconfined quark matter or from hadronic to quarkyonic quark matter. Stable massive cold hybrid stars with only quarkyonic and/or deconfined quark phase are obtained. We systematically examine how the parameters of the modified Polyakov potential and the quark vector interactions control the location of these transitions, and find that repulsive vector interactions are essential to obtain a stable quark core. Hybrid stars with quarkyonic and/or a deconfined core can reach maximum masses above $2M_\odot$, provided a sufficiently stiff hadronic equation of state is used at low density. In the core of the maximum-mass configurations, the speed of sound exceeds the conformal limit, $c_s^2 = 1/3$, for the quarkyonic core stars. This work establishes the qualitative role of each model parameter in shaping hybrid-star structure.

</details>


### [137] [Scalar induced gravitational waves as probes of dark QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12706)
*Wan-Zhe Feng,Ao Li,Jing-Zhi Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量诱导引力波（SIGWs）作为探测暗QCD交叉相变探针的可能性。受孪生希格斯和不对称孪生重子暗物质模型启发，作者考虑了一个禁闭尺度约为标准模型QCD尺度5.5倍的暗QCD扇区，构建其有效能量与熵自由度，求解一阶标量扰动和二阶SIGW，发现暗QCD交叉会在暗禁闭尺度附近产生额外的频移畸变特征，可作为暗QCD扇区的独特信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索间接探测隐藏禁闭扇区的新途径。传统直接探测手段难以触及暗QCD等隐藏强相互作用扇区，而引力波作为纯粹的引力探针，不受隐藏扇区弱耦合限制，可能提供互补的宇宙学观测手段。

Method: 1) 基于孪生希格斯和不对称孪生重子暗物质模型，构建含SM加暗QCD扇区（含三种或六种轻暗夸克味）的有效能量/熵自由度。2) 计算状态方程参数和声速。3) 以此为基础求解一阶标量扰动和二阶SIGWs，穿越SM和暗QCD交叉时期。4) 采用单色原初曲率功率谱，对比理想辐射主导情形与真实SM热历史，分析暗QCD交叉产生的频谱畸变。

Result: 研究证明：1) 真实的SM热历史会相对于理想化辐射主导情形修正SIGW谱；2) 暗QCD交叉在增强标量模于暗禁闭尺度附近重入视界时产生额外的频移畸变；3) 该畸变特征可作为暗QCD扇区的特征签名，其频谱特征相对于SM QCD印记方向更高频率偏移。

Conclusion: SIGWs为隐藏禁闭扇区提供了一种互补的宇宙学探针，其特征谱特征可作为暗QCD扇区的独特观测签名。该分析框架可推广至其他包含不同暗禁闭扇区的理论模型。

Abstract: We investigate scalar induced gravitational waves (SIGWs) as probes of a dark QCD crossover. Motivated by twin Higgs and asymmetric twin baryon dark matter scenarios, we consider a dark QCD sector with a confinement scale approximately 5.5 times the Standard Model (SM) QCD scale. We construct the effective energy and entropy degrees of freedom for the SM supplemented by dark QCD sectors containing either three light dark quark flavors or all six dark quark flavors. The resulting equation of state parameter and sound speed are then used to solve the first-order scalar perturbations and the second-order SIGWs through the SM and dark QCD crossover epochs. For a monochromatic primordial curvature power spectrum, we first demonstrate that the realistic SM thermal history modifies the SIGW spectrum relative to the idealized radiation-dominated case. We then show that a dark QCD crossover generates an additional frequency-shifted distortion when the enhanced scalar mode reenters the horizon near the dark confinement scale. This distinctive feature can therefore serve as a characteristic signature of the dark QCD sector. Our results demonstrate that SIGWs provide a complementary cosmological probe of hidden confining sectors, with characteristic spectral features shifted to higher frequencies relative to the SM QCD imprint. The analysis developed in this work can also be extended to other well-motivated theories containing different dark confining sectors.

</details>


### [138] [Topological diagrams of $Ω^0_c$ decays in the $SU(3)_F$ limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12742)
*Ying-Xin Lai,Di Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在SU(3)味对称性极限下研究了Ω_c^0重子衰变的拓扑振幅，完整给出了树图和企鹅图对Ω_c^0衰变到十重态和八重态重子的贡献，并通过张量分析推导了拓扑振幅与SU(3)不可约振幅之间的线性关系，获得了若干同位旋关系以及用于检验Körner-Pati-Woo定理的关系式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ω_c^0重子是独特的粲六重态重子，仅通过弱相互作用衰变。研究其衰变中的拓扑振幅有助于深入理解粲重子弱衰变的动力学机制，并为检验标准模型中的对称性关系（如Körner-Pati-Woo定理）提供理论依据。

Method: 在SU(3)_F极限下，对Ω_c^0衰变到十重态和八重态重子的过程进行拓扑振幅分析。完整列出树图和企鹅图贡献，并通过张量分析方法推导拓扑振幅与SU(3)不可约振幅之间的线性对应关系，进而导出同位旋关系及检验Körner-Pati-Woo定理的关系式。

Result: 完整呈现了Ω_c^0衰变到十重态和八重态重子的树图和企鹅图拓扑振幅；推导出拓扑振幅与SU(3)不可约振幅的线性关系；获得了若干同位旋关系，并得到了一些可用于检验Körner-Pati-Woo定理的关系式。

Conclusion: 该工作为Ω_c^0弱衰变提供了系统的拓扑振幅框架，所导出的同位旋关系和对Körner-Pati-Woo定理的检验关系有望在未来的实验测量中得到验证，从而加深对粲重子弱衰变机制的理解。

Abstract: The $Ω^0_c$ baryon is a unique charmed sextet baryon as it decays through weak interaction. In this work, we investigate the topological amplitudes of $Ω_c^0$ decays in the $SU(3)_F$ limit. The tree- and penguin-induced diagrams contributing to $Ω_c^0$ decays into decuplet and octet baryons are presented completely. The linear relations between the topological amplitudes and the $SU(3)$ irreducible amplitudes are derived via tensor analysis. Several isospin relations are obtained, and some relations are derived to test the Körner-Pati-Woo theorem.

</details>


### [139] [In-medium properties of $D$ and $D^*$ mesons in magnetized isospin asymmetric nuclear matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12834)
*Saksham Sharma,Dhananjay Singh,Suneel Dutt,Harleen Dahiya,Arvind Kumar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在等旋不对称核物质中，结合手征SU(3)夸克平均场(CQMF)模型与光前夸克模型(LFQM)的混合框架，系统研究了外磁场对赝标量(D^0,D^+,D_s)和矢量(D^{0*},D^{+*},D_s^*)粲介子介质性质的影响，发现磁场通过磁催化增强介子有效质量和衰变常数，而重子密度增加则引起吸引性质量移动并抑制衰变常数和分布振幅，朗道量子化进一步提升带电介子质量，赝标量-矢量混合产生能级排斥效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用物质中重味介子在极端环境（强磁场、高密度、有限温度）下的介质行为，为FAIR、NICA和J-PARC等实验设施中的重夸克动力学研究提供理论预测和参考。

Method: 采用混合理论框架：首先用CQMF模型计算介质修正的组分夸克质量（包含磁化Dirac海贡献和核子反常磁矩），然后将其作为输入到LFQM中计算介子质量、弱衰变常数和前导扭度分布振幅；同时纳入限制在最低朗道能级的带电介子朗道量子化效应，以及每个D-D*双重态的磁致赝标量-矢量混合。

Result: 外部磁场通过磁催化增强赝标量和矢量D介子的有效质量与衰变常数；增加重子密度产生吸引性质量移动并抑制衰变常数和分布振幅；朗道能级贡献进一步提升带电介子有效质量；赝标量-矢量混合产生能级排斥（矢量介子质量上移、赝标量介子质量下移）；等旋不对称性在所有介子态中诱导出小而系统的质量劈裂。

Conclusion: 磁场与密度效应的相互作用导致重轻介子产生非平凡的介质行为，这些结果为强相互作用物质中的重味动力学提供了有价值的见解，对FAIR、NICA和J-PARC的当前及未来研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of an external magnetic field on the in-medium properties of pseudoscalar ($D^0,D^+,D_s$) and vector ($D^{0*},D^{+*},D_{s}^{*}$) mesons in isospin asymmetric nuclear matter at finite temperature using a hybrid theoretical framework combining the chiral SU(3) quark mean-field (CQMF) model and the light-front quark model (LFQM). The medium-modified constituent quark masses, obtained from the CQMF model by including the magnetized Dirac sea contribution and anomalous magnetic moments of nucleons, are used as input to the LFQM calculations of meson masses, weak decay constants, and leading-twist distribution amplitudes. We further incorporate the Landau quantization of the charged mesons restricted to the lowest Landau level, while magnetic field induced pseudoscalar-vector mixing is taken into account for each $D$-$D^{*}$ doublet. We find that the external magnetic field enhances the effective masses and decay constants of both pseudoscalar and vector $D$ mesons by magnetic catalysis, while increasing baryon density generally induces an attractive mass shift and suppresses the decay constant and distribution amplitudes. The Landau level contribution further enhances the effective masses of the charged mesons, whereas the pseudoscalar-vector mixing produces a level repulsion, shifting the vector meson masses upward and the pseudoscalar meson masses downward. The interplay between magnetic field and density effects gives rise to a nontrivial medium behavior of heavy-light meson properties, with isospin asymmetry further inducing a small but systematic mass splitting across all the meson states considered. These results provide useful insights into heavy-flavor dynamics in strongly interacting matter and are relevant to ongoing and future studies at FAIR, NICA, and J-PARC.

</details>


### [140] [Entropy from inclusive scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12923)
*M. A. Braun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在无相互作用（即无pomeron间耦合）的幺正pomeron交换模型中，如何从多个包含截面引入概率，并发现由概率导出的非弹性截面与幺正性要求存在偏差，该偏差随能量增加而减小并在高能极限下消失。数值计算了概率与熵，发现熵随能量先上升，在快度12-17处达到最大值后缓慢下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无pomeron相互作用的幺正pomeron交换模型中，从多个包含截面定义概率的一致性，检验其与幺正性约束的符合程度，并探究高能极限下的行为及熵的能量依赖性。

Method: 在无pomeron间相互作用的幺正pomeron交换模型框架下，从多个包含截面定义概率，并与幺正性导出的非弹性截面进行对比；对概率和熵进行数值计算，考察其随能量（快度）的变化。

Result: 概率导出的非弹性截面与幺正性结果存在偏差，但该偏差随能量升高而减小并最终消失。熵随能量非单调变化：先上升，在快度12-17处达到峰值，随后缓慢下降。

Conclusion: 在无pomeron相互作用的模型中，概率与幺正性在高能极限下趋于一致；熵的能量依赖行为呈现非平凡的峰结构，与朴素预期不同。

Abstract: Introduction of probabilities from multiple inclusive cross-sections is studied
  in the unitary pomeron exchange models with no interaction between the pomerons themselves.
  Discrepancy is observed between the emerging inelasic cross-section and the one following from unitarity.
  It diminishes with energy and disappears in the high-energy limit. Probabilities and
  entropy are calculated numerically. Contrary to naive predictions as a function of energy the entropy first rises and after achievung its maximum at rapidity 12-17 slowly falls for higher energies

</details>


### [141] [Newtrinos.jl: A Julia Package for Global Analysis of Neutrino Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12940)
*Philipp Eller,David Schultheiß*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Newtrinos.jl是一个开源的Julia包，用于中微子数据的全局分析，采用模块化三层架构（物理模型、实验描述、统计推断分离），支持频率学派和贝叶斯推断，可组合构建多数据集联合似然，并支持自动微分与并行计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子物理的全局分析需要灵活组合不同实验数据和理论模型，但现有工具通常将实验描述、物理模型和统计推断耦合在一起，难以扩展和复用。

Method: 采用模块化三层架构，将物理模型、实验描述和统计推断分离为独立模块；每个实验数据集实现完整的统计前向模型（包括所有相关系统不确定性），定义似然和数据生成过程；支持参数跨实验合并/相关/去相关；提供频率学派和贝叶斯推断方法；利用Julia的自动微分实现精确梯度计算，支持CPU线程和分布式并行。

Result: 该框架实现了可组合的联合似然构建，新实验和物理模型可无需修改核心代码即可添加，并支持现代统计推断工作流（频率学派与贝叶斯），在Julia生态中实现全自动微分和并行化。

Conclusion: Newtrinos.jl通过模块化架构和完整的统计前向建模，为中微子数据的全局分析提供了一个灵活、可扩展且计算高效的框架。

Abstract: Newtrinos.jl is an open-source Julia package for performing global analyses of neutrino data. It provides a modular, three-layer architecture that separates physics models, experiment descriptions, and statistical inference into independent modules. This allows researchers to freely combine experiments and test them against a variety of theoretical models. New experiments and physics models can be added without modifying core code. Full statistical forward models including all relevant systematic uncertainties are implemented for each experimental dataset, defining both the likelihood and the data-generating process and enabling modern statistical inference workflows. The framework is composable, making it straightforward to construct joint likelihoods over multiple datasets. Physics and nuisance parameters can be merged, correlated, or decorrelated across experiments to ensure consistency in the joint fit. The package supports Frequentist and Bayesian inference and is parallelizable across CPU threads or distributed workers. Written entirely in Julia, all models are automatically differentiable, enabling exact gradient computation.

</details>


### [142] [Decay widths of radially excited vector mesons of the $U(3)$ nonet with production of three pseudoscalar mesons in the extended NJL model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13140)
*M. K. Volkov,A. A. Pivovarov,K. Nurlan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在扩展NJL模型中计算了第一径向激发矢量介子ρ'、ω'、φ'和K*'衰变产生3个赝标量介子（K、π、η）的过程，考虑了箱图通道以及含基态和第一径向激发态中间矢量介子的通道，结果为未来实验提供了预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究第一径向激发矢量介子的三体衰变过程，为这些粒子的性质提供理论预言，并为未来实验（如BESIII、Belle等）提供可验证的参考。

Method: 采用扩展的NJL（Nambu-Jona-Lasinio）模型，计算ρ'、ω'、φ'和K*'衰变到三个赝标量介子（K、π、η）的过程。考虑了两类贡献：箱图（box）通道和含有基态及第一径向激发态中间矢量介子的通道。

Result: 计算得到了这些第一径向激发矢量介子衰变到三个赝标量介子的宽度和分支比，结果可作为未来实验检验的理论预测。

Conclusion: 该工作在扩展NJL模型的框架下系统计算了第一径向激发矢量介子的三赝标量介子衰变，为未来高能物理实验提供了明确的理论预言。

Abstract: In the extended NJL model, the decays of the first radially excited vector mesons $ρ'$,$ω'$, $φ'$ and ${K^*}'$ with production of $3P$, where $P=K,π,η$ are calculated. The box channels and the channels with intermediate vector mesons in the ground and first radially excited states are considered. The obtained results can be considered as predictions for future experiments.

</details>


### [143] [Magnetization and Magnetic Field-Induced Correction: Implications for QGP Thermal Photon Production in Magnetohydrodynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13364)
*Jing Jing,Duan She,Ze-Fang Jiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在(1+1)维相对论磁流体力学(MHD)框架内系统研究了磁化夸克-胶子等离子体(QGP)的热光子发射，涵盖磁化率（包括常数和格点QCD温度依赖形式）以及弱场量子修正对夸克分布函数的影响，发现光子产额主要由初始磁场强度及其时间衰减决定，磁化率影响可忽略，而弱场修正可在中等p_T区域增强热光子产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高能重离子碰撞中，强磁场影响QGP的电磁可观测量，需要建立包含磁化效应和量子修正的热光子发射理论框架，为关联磁化QGP动力学与电磁信号提供定量基础。

Method: 采用Pu-Bjorken MHD框架，系统纳入磁化率χ_m（常数及格点QCD温度依赖参数化）和弱场量子修正f_EM，计算康普顿散射、q-anti-q湮灭、轫致辐射及带重散射湮灭等过程的光子产生率，并沿QGP时空演化积分得到横动量谱。

Result: 光子产额主要由初始磁场强度和磁场时间衰减轮廓主导，磁化率χ_m在研究的参数空间内影响可忽略；而弱场修正f_EM在中等p_T区域引起热光子产生的显著增强。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁化QGP中电磁可观测量的定量计算建立了严格的理论框架，并为未来包含自旋-磁化动力学的耗散MHD研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate thermal photon emission from magnetized quark-gluon plasma (QGP) within (1+1)-dimensional relativistic magnetohydrodynamics (MHD), systematically incorporating magnetic susceptibility $χ_m$---encompassing both constant and lattice-QCD-derived temperature-dependent $χ_m(T)$ parametrizations---and weak-field quantum corrections to quark distribution functions $f_{\rm EM}$. Employing the Pu-Bjorken MHD framework, we calculate photon production rates from Compton scattering, $q\bar{q}$ annihilation, bremsstrahlung, and annihilation with rescattering, and integrate these over the QGP spacetime evolution to obtain transverse momentum ($p_T$) spectra. Our results demonstrate that photon yields are predominantly governed by the initial magnetic field strength and its temporal decay profile, with $χ_m$ exerting negligible influence in the explored parameter space. In contrast, the weak-field correction $f_{\rm EM}$ induces a distinct enhancement in thermal photon production at intermediate $p_T$. This work establishes a rigorous theoretical framework for quantifying electromagnetic observables in magnetized QGP and provides the foundation for future dissipative MHD studies incorporating spin-magnetization dynamics.

</details>


### [144] [Non-perturbative quark production and transport in the evolving glasma: the WAGASHI event generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13393)
*Nicholas J. Benoit,Chiho Nonaka,Hidetoshi Taya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了WAGASHI模型，将经典Yang-Mills glasma演化、Wong方程输运和Schwinger对产生结合在逐事件基础上，研究相对论重离子碰撞早期glasma阶段的非微扰夸克-反夸克对产生及后续夸克动力学。研究发现glasma阶段已产生与最终强子产额相当数量的夸克，并经历显著的动量展宽和自旋随机化，表明对QGP早期热化有重要贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相对论重离子碰撞中夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）的早期热化机制，特别是glasma阶段非微扰夸克对产生及其对后续流体动力学演化的影响。

Method: 构建WAGASHI模型，整合三个要素：(1)经典Yang-Mills glasma演化；(2)Wong方程描述夸克在色场中的输运；(3)Schwinger机制描述夸克-反夸克对产生。模型在逐事件（event-by-event）基础上运行，适用于Pb-Pb和O-O碰撞系统。

Result: 在glasma阶段已产生大量夸克（数量与最终强子产额相当），这些夸克经历显著的动量展宽和自旋随机化。确定了Pb-Pb和O-O碰撞中重子数、电荷、奇异数和自旋极化的逐事件分布，发现较小的O-O系统中存在特别大的涨落。

Conclusion: glasma阶段产生的夸克对QGP早期热化有显著贡献，该模型提供了可用于后续流体动力学演化模拟的动力初始条件。

Abstract: We study non-perturbative quark-antiquark pair production and the subsequent quark dynamics in the earliest glasma stage of relativistic heavy-ion collisions. For that, we develop a new model, WAGASHI (Wong-precessing Anisotropic Glasma And ScHwinger-produced Initial-conditions), which combines classical Yang-Mills glasma evolution, Wong-equation transport, and Schwinger pair production on an event-by-event basis. We find that a sizeable number of quarks, comparable to the final hadron yields, are produced already during the glasma stage and subsequently undergo substantial momentum broadening and spin randomization, suggesting a significant contribution toward the early equilibration of the quark-gluon plasma. We also determine the event-by-event distributions of baryon number, electric charge, strangeness, and spin polarization in Pb-Pb and O-O collisions at LHC energies, finding particularly large fluctuations in the smaller O-O system. These results provide dynamical initial conditions for the subsequent hydrodynamic evolution of the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [145] [Critical behavior and critical exponents of rotating QCD matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13469)
*Kai Xiao,Fei Sun,Shuang Li,Xun Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在平均场近似下，利用两味Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL)模型研究了旋转强相互作用物质的热力学性质与临界行为，确定了温度-角速度平面上的相结构和临界端点(CEP)，并提取了表征比热密度、转动极化不连续、转动磁化率及临界等温行为的有效临界指数。所得指数接近预期的平均场值并满足标度关系，表明转动自由度不改变平均场临界标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旋转强相互作用物质（如重离子碰撞中产生的涡旋夸克-胶子物质）的热力学性质和临界现象，系统表征转动诱导的临界行为，为超越平均场近似的旋转QCD物质研究奠定基础。

Method: 采用两味NJL模型，在平均场近似框架下，通过分析热力学观测量在CEP附近的奇异行为，提取比热密度、转动极化不连续、转动磁化率和临界等温行为对应的有效临界指数。

Result: 得到的临界指数趋近于预期的平均场值，并满足相应的标度关系，表明转动自由度在该框架内不改变潜在的均场临界标度行为。

Conclusion: 研究提供了转动诱导临界现象的系统表征，建立了超越平均场近似的旋转QCD物质进一步研究的基础。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic properties and critical behavior of rotating strongly interacting matter within the two-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model in the mean-field approximation. The phase structure and the critical endpoint (CEP) are determined in the temperature--angular velocity \((T,ω)\) plane. By analyzing the singular behavior of thermodynamic observables near the CEP, we extract the corresponding effective critical exponents characterizing the scaling behavior of the specific heat density, the rotational polarization discontinuity, the rotational susceptibility, and the critical-isotherm behavior of the rotational polarization. The obtained exponents approach the expected mean-field values and satisfy the corresponding scaling relations, indicating that the rotational degree of freedom does not alter the underlying mean-field critical scaling behavior within the present framework. These results provide a systematic characterization of rotation-induced critical phenomena and establish a basis for further studies of rotating QCD matter beyond the mean-field approximation.

</details>


### [146] [A pion-driven near-threshold enhancement in the $π\,T_{cc}$ system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13525)
*Pedro Brandão,Jorgivan Morais Dias,Luciano M. Abreu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了T_cc^+(3875)（一个实验确认的强子分子态）能否作为构建更复杂奇特强子的基本单元。通过在Faddeev方程的固定中心近似下分析π散射T_cc的过程，发现在πT_cc阈值附近存在一个中心质量约4055 MeV、表观宽度约60 MeV的共振态增强结构。这证明了最轻的强子（π介子）可以发挥积极的动力学作用，使已观测到的分子态成为更重近阈值结构的基本单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强子分子态通常被视为近阈值二体动力学的产物。一个自然且重要的科学问题是：已实验确立的分子态能否作为构建更复杂奇特强子的构建单元？这关系到对强子谱结构的理解以及奇特强子态之间层次关系的探索。

Method: 将T_cc视为关联的等标量DD*分子簇，在I(J^P)=1(1^-)道中利用Faddeev方程的固定中心近似（Fixed Center Approximation）描述πDD*三体动力学。通过数值求解三体振幅来研究π散射T_cc的过程。

Result: 完整的三体振幅在πT_cc阈值略上方出现显著增强，峰中心M_peak≈4055 MeV，表观宽度约60 MeV。振幅实部和虚部的行为与类共振解释相容。

Conclusion: 结果表明最轻的强子（π）可以发挥积极的动力学作用，将观测到的强子分子态促进为更重近阈值结构的基本单元。建议在T_ccπ不变量质量分布中寻找该增强结构，尤其通过D^0D^0π^+π^-末态进行探测。

Abstract: Hadronic molecules are commonly viewed as products of near-threshold two-body dynamics. We investigate whether an experimentally established molecule can also serve as a building block for a more complex exotic hadron by studying pion scattering off $T^+_{cc}(3875)$. Treating $T_{cc}$ as a correlated isoscalar $D D^*$ molecular cluster, we describe the resulting $πD D^*$ dynamics in the $I(J^P)=1(1^-)$ channel within the Fixed Center Approximation to the Faddeev equations. The full three-body amplitude develops a pronounced enhancement slightly above the $πT_{cc}$ threshold, centered at $M_{\rm peak}\simeq 4055$~MeV, with an apparent width of approximately $60$~MeV. The associated behavior of the real and imaginary parts of the amplitude is compatible with a resonant-like interpretation. These results show that the lightest hadron can play an active dynamical role in promoting an observed hadronic molecule into a building block of a heavier near-threshold structure generated by the three-body dynamics. We propose searching for this enhancement in the $T_{cc}π$ invariant-mass distribution, particularly through the $D^0D^0π^+π^-$ final state.

</details>


### [147] [Kinematic Fingerprints of a Nucleon-Triggered $V_{\mathcal{B}}$ in Rare $η^{(\prime)}\toπ^0(η)γγ$ Decays on Nucleon Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13537)
*Yaroslav Balytskyi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了由核子触发的矢量玻色子模型$V_{\mathcal B}$对$\eta\to\pi^0\gamma\gamma$衰变的影响。该模型被提出以解释KLOE实验测量与以MAMI光产生数据为主的世界平均值之间的显著性差异。研究发现MAMI与JEF两类实验条件下预测存在微小差异，最干净的判别特征是运动学禁戒尾部区域，预测在1200个样本中出现4-23个事件；两能量区质量偏移相似（约2-7 MeV），并初步识别了可能解释γd→π⁰ηd和γd→π⁰π⁰d角分布异常的候选拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KLOE对$\eta\to\pi^0\gamma\gamma$分支比的测量结果$(0.98\pm0.11\pm0.14)\times10^{-4}$与世界平均值$(2.55\pm0.22)\times10^{-4}$（主要由MAMI光产生数据主导）之间存在显著统计矛盾，需提出新物理模型解释这一差异。核子触发的矢量玻色子模型$V_{\mathcal B}$是一个候选方案，其特点是新相互作用仅在存在外部核子时激活，因此不影响KLOE和BESIII等轻子型测量。

Method: 本文定量计算了$V_{\mathcal B}$模型下由于MAMI与JEF不同产生能量区间所导致的残余运动学与截断依赖效应；分析了运动学禁戒尾部（$\gamma\gamma$和$\pi^0\gamma$谱）中的信号事件数及其与反冲质子运动学的关联；比较了相同选择窗口下两能量体制的有效分支比差异；计算了重构质量$M_{\pi^0\gamma\gamma}$的平均上移；并识别了$V_{\mathcal B}$诱导的、可能解释γd→π⁰ηd和γd→π⁰π⁰d角分布异常的拓扑结构。

Result: 在1200个样本中预测禁戒尾部区域有4-23个事件（取决于选择窗口）；对壳假设下极角偏差在MAMI能量可达约-5°以下，而JEF仅约-0.2°；相同选择窗口下JEF有效分支比仅比MAMI预测小小于1%，加宽窗口可使两者增加约4%；两能量区平均重构质量上移均为$\langle M_{\pi^0\gamma\gamma}\rangle-m_\eta\simeq 2$–7 MeV。

Conclusion: $V_{\mathcal B}$模型的干净判别器是运动学禁戒尾部的存在及其与反冲质子运动学的关联；两能量体制虽细节略有差异但整体预测一致，需通过禁戒尾部搜索来验证模型；同时该模型可能为γd→π⁰ηd和γd→π⁰π⁰d角分布异常提供新物理解释，但定量研究留待后续工作。

Abstract: The nucleon-triggered vector-boson scenario $V_{\mathcal B}$ was motivated by a statistically significant discrepancy between the KLOE measurement, $\mathrm{BR}^{η\rightarrowπ^{0}γγ}_{\text{KLOE}} = \left(0.98\pm0.11_{\text{stat}}\pm0.14_{\text{syst}}\right)\times10^{-4}$, and the current world average, $\left(2.55\pm0.22\right)\times10^{-4}$, dominated by MAMI photoproduction data. In this model, the new interaction is activated by external nucleons, which causes deviations from the Standard Model predictions, while leaving leptonic measurements, such as KLOE and BESIII, unaffected. We quantify the residual kinematic and cut-dependent effects that make the MAMI- and JEF-like predictions close but not identical due to their different production-energy regimes. The cleanest discriminator of the model is a kinematically forbidden tail in the $γγ$ and $π^0γ$ spectra, and for $η\rightarrowπ^0γγ$, and we predict 4--23 events in a sample of 1200, depending on the selection window, in these regions. These events correlate with the kinematics of the recoil proton, and the polar angle relative to the on-shell hypothesis can reach $\approx-5^\circ$ or below at MAMI energies, but only $\approx -0.2^\circ$ at JEF. For identical selection windows, the JEF-like effective branching fraction exceeds the MAMI-like prediction by less than $1\%$, while widening the window increases both predictions by up to $\sim4\%$. Both energy regimes produce a similar upward shift of the mean reconstructed mass, $\langle M_{π^0γγ}\rangle-m_η\simeq 2$--$7~\mathrm{MeV}$. Finally, we identify representative $V_{\mathcal B}$-induced topologies that could contribute to recently reported discrepancies in the angular distributions of $γd\toπ^0ηd$ and $γd\toπ^0π^0 d$, leaving their quantitative investigation to future work.

</details>

### [148] [Categorification of the genus two DAHA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13278)
*Semeon Arthamonov,Ievgen Makedonskyi,Daniil Sarafannikov*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了平坦退化D(2|1,α)当前代数的分级可积表示的导出范畴上的三个导出自函子，并证明它们在Grothendieck群中的类即为亏格2的Macdonald算子。通过显式计算分次Ext配对，推导出亏格2的Macdonald算子的自伴性以及亏格2的Macdonald多项式关于某一斜双线性形式的正交性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在通过表示论与导出范畴的工具，将Macdonald算子与多项式推广到亏格2的情形。Macdonald多项式在代数组合学、表示论和数学物理中具有核心地位，将其推广到更高亏格并建立其算子的自伴性与正交性，是组合表示论中的一个重要方向。利用李代数的导出范畴构造Macdonald算子，提供了一种范畴化的视角。

Method: 作者在平坦退化D(2|1,α)的当前代数的分级可积表示的导出范畴上，构造了三个导出自函子，并在Grothendieck群中研究其类。通过显式地计算分次Ext配对，验证这些算子类满足亏格2 Macdonald算子所满足的代数关系，进而分析其自伴性和对应多项式的正交性。

Result: 三个导出自函子在Grothendieck群中的类恰好是亏格2的Macdonald算子；通过分次Ext配对的显式计算，证明了亏格2的Macdonald算子关于某斜双线性形式是自伴的，且亏格2的Macdonald多项式在该形式下相互正交。

Conclusion: 该工作将Macdonald理论推广到亏格2，建立了导出范畴中的函子构造与组合Macdonald算子之间的桥梁，并给出了亏格2情形下算子自伴性与多项式正交性的严格证明，为更高亏格的推广奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: We construct three derived endofunctors on the derived category of graded integrable representations of the current algebra of a flat degeneration of $D(2|1,α)$, and prove that their classes in the Grothendieck group are the genus two Macdonald operators. Computing the graded $\operatorname{Ext}$ pairing explicitly, we deduce the self-adjointness of the genus two Macdonald operators and the orthogonality of genus $2$ Macdonald polynomials with respect to the certain skew-bilinear form.

</details>


### [149] [Langlands Duality and Invariant Differential Operators: the Case SL(2n+1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13400)
*V. K. Dobrev*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Langlands对偶性与不变微分算子之间的桥梁构建，聚焦于SL(2n+1)群的情况，与之前研究的SL(2n)群形成对比，尽管两组相似但其表示理论存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Langlands对偶性是数学研究中极具影响力的课题，而不变微分算子领域在哈里斯-钱德拉半单李群表示理论中同样根基深厚，但两者此前似乎未有联系。作者旨在建立这两者之间的桥梁。

Method: 作者先在SL(2n)群的情况下开始了桥梁构建工作，本论文则将研究扩展到SL(2n+1)群的情况。由于两个群虽然相似但表示理论差异较大，需要对SL(2n+1)的情况进行专门处理。

Result: 本文尚未在摘要中给出具体结果，但表明已完成对SL(2n+1)群情况的处理，并将其与先前SL(2n)的工作进行对比分析。

Conclusion: 通过研究SL(2n+1)群的不变微分算子，作者正在建立其与Langlands对偶性之间的联系，弥补这一长期存在的空白。SL(2n)和SL(2n+1)表示理论的差异为这一桥梁构建提供了新的视角和挑战。

Abstract: Recently we started building a bridge between two cases of Langlands duality. The latter is one of the most influential topics in mathematical research. It has many different appearances and influential subtopics. Yet there is a topic that until now seems unrelated to the Langlands program. That is the topic of invariant differential operators. That is strange since both items are deeply rooted in Harish-Chandra's representation theory of semisimple Lie groups.
  We started with the case of the group ~$SL(2n)$. In the present paper we deal with the group $SL(2n+1)$. The two mentioned groups are similar, but their representation theories is rather different.

</details>


### [150] [Nilpotent orbits of sl(n,R) admit no Gibbs state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12608)
*Jérémie Pierard de Maujouy,Guillaume Neuttiens*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究SL(n,R)的幂零轨道的Gibbs态存在性问题，证明了当n>2时这些轨道不承认任何Gibbs态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs态是在Hamiltonian G-流形上定义的概率分布，由李代数g中的开集参数化，是与群G作用相关的自然平衡态。研究者希望探讨SL(n,R)的幂零轨道是否能够承载这类平衡态。

Method: 本文通过分析SL(n,R)在Hamiltonian G-流形上的作用结构，聚焦于幂零轨道这一特定类别，并对其是否存在Gibbs态进行严格数学论证。

Result: 证明了对于n>2的情况，SL(n,R)的幂零轨道不承认任何Gibbs态。

Conclusion: SL(n,R)的幂零轨道在n>2时不具备Gibbs态的存在性，这一结果揭示了此类轨道在平衡态理论中的特殊地位。

Abstract: Gibbs states are probability distributions defined on Hamiltonian G-manifolds. They are parametrized by an open set of the Lie algebra g and arise as natural equilibrium states with regard to the action of G on the system. In this paper, we focus on nilpotent orbits of SL(n,R). We show that for n>2, those orbits do not admit any Gibbs state.

</details>


### [151] [Minimal Nilpotent Orbits of type G2, F4 and E8](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12985)
*Boming Jia*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于类型为G_2、F_4、E_8的复单李代数g，其极小幂零轨道闭包\overline{\mathcal O}_{\min}(g)不可能是任何光滑拟仿射簇X的余切丛仿射化(T^*X)^{\mathrm{aff}}，且该结论不依赖于等变或Poisson同构假设。同时证明了A_1和A_2类型的相同结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究幂零轨道闭包与余切丛仿射化之间的同构关系，是辛几何与代数几何交叉领域的重要问题。此前相关结果可能依赖于等变或Poisson结构的假设，作者旨在去除这些额外条件，证明即使在更弱的非等变、非Poisson意义下，某些例外类型李代数的极小幂零轨道闭包仍不能表示为光滑拟仿射簇的余切丛仿射化。

Method: 本文针对复单李代数类型G_2、F_4、E_8（以及A_1、A_2），在不假设同构为等变或Poisson的普遍框架下，利用代数几何与辛几何的性质（如奇点结构、余切丛仿射化的不变量等），通过反证法或分类论证来排除同构的存在性。

Result: 证明了在G_2、F_4、E_8类型中，极小幂零轨道闭包\overline{\mathcal O}_{\min}(\mathfrak g)与任何光滑拟仿射簇X的(T^*X)^{\mathrm{aff}}不同构，且该证明不依赖等变或Poisson假设；此外在A_1和A_2类型中也得到了同样的结论。

Conclusion: 该结果深化了对幂零轨道闭包几何结构的理解，表明在多个李代数类型中，极小幂零轨道闭包无法在非等变的宽松意义下实现为光滑拟仿射簇余切丛的仿射化，这为后续研究更广泛类型的幂零轨道闭包的结构提供了新的约束与方法。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak g$ be a complex simple Lie algebra of type $G_2$, $F_4$, or $E_8$. We prove that the closure $\overline{\mathcal O}_{\min}(\mathfrak g)$ of its minimal nilpotent orbit is not isomorphic to $(T^*X)^{\mathrm{aff}}$ for any smooth quasi-affine variety $X$. We do not assume that the isomorphism is equivariant or Poisson. We also prove the same statement in types $A_1$ and $A_2$.

</details>


### [152] [Absolutely flat algebras in tensor categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13137)
*Kevin Coulembier,Alexander Sherman*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了张量范畴（刚性阿贝尔幺半范畴）的ind-完备化中半单代数、阿廷单代数和阿廷绝对平坦代数之间的关系，证明在适度增长的对称张量范畴中（若接受该领域的主要猜想则为所有此类范畴），这三类代数在交换代数情形下是一致的；同时给出反例表明在一般情况下这些概念会分化，不同于域上代数的经典情形。附录中还首次给出了具有有限维态射空间但对象无限长的张量范畴的例子，并推广了Deligne关于tannakian范畴在代数闭域上中性的定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典代数中，域上的半单代数、阿廷单代数和阿廷绝对平坦代数之间有着紧密且明确的关系。然而在张量范畴这一更广泛的框架下，这些概念的行为尚不清晰。作者旨在系统研究这三类代数在张量范畴的ind-完备化中的关系，既探讨在什么条件下它们重合，也揭示在什么情况下它们分道扬镳，从而建立该领域的基础性理解。

Method: 作者系统分析了张量范畴ind-完备化中各类代数的结构，将代数性质与范畴的对称性和增长条件联系起来。通过区分适度增长与一般情形，利用范畴论技术证明在适度增长对称张量范畴中（条件良好时）三类代数重合；同时构造具体反例展示一般情形下的分歧。附录中运用范畴论构造给出有限维态射空间但对象无限长的张量范畴实例，并推广Deligne定理至更一般的范畴框架。

Result: 主要结果表明：在适度增长的对称张量范畴中（若接受该领域主要猜想则为所有此类范畴），交换的半单代数、阿廷单代数和阿廷绝对平坦代数三者重合。然而在一般情况下，通过反例证明这些概念确实会分化。附录给出了第一个有限维态射空间但对象无限长的张量范畴示例，并将Deligne中性化定理推广到代数闭域上的更一般情形。

Conclusion: 本文厘清了张量范畴背景下三类代数的关系：在良好条件下（适度增长、交换情形）三者等价，但超出该范围后它们不再一致，这显著区别于经典的域上代数理论。附录中的构造和定理推广为张量范畴理论提供了新的工具和更深入的理解。

Abstract: We study the relation between semisimple algebras, artinian simple algebras, and artinian absolutely flat algebras in ind-completions of tensor categories (rigid abelian monoidal categories). Our main results show that the three types of algebras coincide, if we restrict to commutative algebras in certain (all, if we accept the main conjectures in the field) symmetric tensor categories of moderate growth. We also provide examples to show that in general these notions tend to diverge, contrary to the classical case of algebras over a field. In one appendix we give the first, to the best of our knowledge, example of a tensor category with finite-dimensional morphism spaces but objects of infinite length. In a second appendix we establish a generalisation of Deligne's theorem that tannakian categories are neutral over algebraically closed fields.

</details>


### [153] [$g$-vector fans and picture categories for 0-Auslander extriangulated categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13175)
*Erlend D. Børve,Maximilian Kaipel*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文将g-向量扇形推广到带有投射silting对象的Hom-finite Krull-Schmidt 0-Auslander k-线性extriangulated范畴上，证明其具有由厚子范畴诱导的容许划分，从而定义picture范畴，并建立厚子范畴与τ-垂直子范畴之间的双射，统一了此前所有picture范畴和τ-簇态射范畴的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将g-向量扇形和picture范畴的概念从有限维代数推广到更一般的extriangulated范畴，统一已有构造，并建立不同代数与范畴之间picture范畴的函数关系框架。

Method: 在Hom-finite Krull-Schmidt 0-Auslander k-线性extriangulated范畴中定义g-向量扇形；利用厚子范畴构造容许划分；通过presilting对象生成的厚子范畴与T的端射k-代数的τ-垂直子范畴建立双射；引入划分扇形的态射作为统一框架。

Result: 建立了g-向量扇形在更一般范畴类中的定义及其容许划分的存在性，证明了厚子范畴与τ-垂直子范畴之间的双射对应，并表明该构造统一了此前所有picture范畴和τ-簇态射范畴的构造。

Conclusion: 该工作将picture范畴理论推广至extriangulated范畴，为不同代数与范畴间picture范畴的函数关系提供了统一框架，为该领域的进一步研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We extend the notion of $\mathbf{g}$-vector fan so that it is defined for a Hom-finite Krull-Schmidt 0-Auslander $k$-linear extriangulated category $\mathcal{C}$ with a projective silting object $T$. Moreover, we show that the $\mathbf{g}$-vector fan admits an admissible partition, in the sense of the second-named author, which is induced by thick subcategories. One can thus define the picture category of $\mathcal{C}$. We establish a bijection between thick subcategories of $\mathcal{C}$ generated by presilting objects containing all projective-injective objects and $τ$-perpendicular subcategories of the endomorphism $k$-algebra of $T$. This shows that our construction unifies all previous constructions of picture categories and $τ$-cluster morphism categories of finite-dimensional algebras. We introduce morphisms of partitioned fans to provide a common framework for the functorial relationships between picture categories of different algebras and categories.

</details>


### [154] [Homogeneous Weights on Semigroup Algebras of Finite Commutative Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13180)
*M. H. Shahzamanian*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上扭转换收缩半群代数的行列式性质，将Steinberg的行列式理论扩展到该框架，建立了Frobenius判别准则，并开发了二元生成扭转换收缩幺半群代数的结构理论。研究发现某些类别的行列式仅依赖于零-非零模式而与上循环值无关，并基于此构造了Frobenius扭转换收缩半群代数上的齐次权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限半群的行列式与其对应半群代数的Frobenius性质密切相关，且在编码理论中有重要应用。作者旨在将Steinberg关于半群代数的行列式理论推广到扭转换（twisted）收缩半群代数这一更一般的情形，并探索其结构性质与齐次权重的构造。

Method: 论文首先在有限域上建立了扭转换收缩半群代数的Frobenius判别准则（Frobenius criterion），推广了Steinberg的行列式理论。其次发展了二元生成扭转换收缩幺半群代数的结构理论，通过识别合适的代数类别，证明了其行列式仅取决于零-非零模式而与上循环（cocycle）取值无关。这意味着只需验证平凡特征下的行列式条件即可推导出所有特征的结果。在这些假设下，半群可由少量定义恒等式完全刻画。最后，受有限Frobenius环上齐次权重的特征构造启发，为Frobenius扭转换收缩半群代数构造了齐次权重，并研究了非Frobenius情形，刻画了特征平均权重满足齐次权重平均性质的理想。

Result: 成功将Steinberg行列式理论推广到扭转换收缩半群代数，建立了Frobenius判别准则；识别出行列式仅取决于零-非零模式（与上循环值无关）的代数类别；构造了Frobenius情形下的齐次权重，并刻画了非Frobenius情形中满足平均性质的主理想族，从而将经典齐次权重概念推广到指定主理想族上。

Conclusion: 本文不仅扩展了扭转换收缩半群代数的行列式理论和Frobenius性质研究，还通过齐次权重的构造建立了代数编码理论的新联系，为后续在更一般代数结构上研究编码理论与行列式理论提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The determinant of a finite semigroup is closely related to the Frobenius property of its corresponding semigroup algebra, and it plays a significant role in coding theory applications. In this paper, we study determinants of twisted contracted semigroup algebras over finite fields. We extend Steinberg's determinant theory to this setting by establishing a Frobenius criterion for twisted contracted semigroup algebras over finite fields. We also develop a structural theory for two-generated twisted contracted monoid algebras. In particular, we identify classes for which the determinant of the associated matrix depends only on its zero--nonzero pattern, and is therefore independent of the values of the cocycle. Consequently, it suffices to verify the determinant condition for the trivial character, from which the corresponding result for all characters follows. Under suitable assumptions, these semigroups are completely determined by a small set of defining identities. Motivated by the character-theoretic construction of homogeneous weights on finite Frobenius rings, we construct homogeneous weights for Frobenius twisted contracted semigroup algebras and investigate the non-Frobenius case. In particular, we characterize the principal ideals on which the character-average weight satisfies the averaging property of homogeneous weights and thereby extend the classical notion of homogeneous weight to a prescribed family of principal ideals.

</details>


### [155] [Moduli of super-representations of quivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13375)
*Sanjay Amrutiya,Umesh Dubey*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该注记将King的几何不变理论（GIT）框架推广到超设置，构建了超表示的模空间，并概述了超箭图簇的构造，将超Grassmannian和超旗簇实现为几何商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的King GIT框架从普通表示范畴推广到超表示范畴，以便在超几何背景下构造模空间，从而为超箭图表示论和超代数几何提供统一的构造工具。

Method: 利用超一般线性群的偶部以及一个奇偶平移算子（parity-shifting operator），扩展King的GIT框架，构造固定超维数超表示的模空间；对于带框架箭图（不做加倍）的超表示，概述超箭图簇的构造；并将超Grassmannian和超旗簇实现为上述框架中的几何商。

Result: 成功构造了超表示的模空间，在拓扑意义上参数化了固定超维数的超表示；实现了超Grassmannian和超旗簇作为该框架下的几何商，并概述了无需加倍过程的超箭图簇构造。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典的GIT商构造系统地推广到超表示理论，为超箭图模空间和超箭图簇提供了统一的构造途径，拓展了几何不变理论的适用范围。

Abstract: In this note, we construct moduli spaces of super-representations of quivers by extending King's Geometric Invariant Theory (GIT) framework to the super setting. Using the even part of super general linear groups and a parity-shifting operator, we construct moduli spaces that topologically parameterize super-representations of a fixed super-dimension.
  We also outline the construction of super quiver varieties for super-representations of framed quivers (without doubling); and realise super-Grassmannians and super-flag varieties as geometric quotients within this setting.

</details>
